Drawing on theories of imitation by Gabriel Tarde and James Mark Baldwin, this paper models creativity as selective transformation across multiple levels of textual representation. We introduce a multi-level framework that compares literary texts across lexical, semantic, conceptual, structural, and narrative dimensions using directional alignment and control calibrated similarity measures. Applying the model to historically documented literary relationships, we show that different pairs preserve source structure at different representational levels while diverging in others. These transformation profiles provide a quantitative method for characterizing how imitation persists and where creative divergence occurs within literary works.

</details>


### [168] [AI systems and the reproduction of (standard) language ideologies in World Englishes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28528)
*Kingsley Ugwuanyi*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文探讨了大型语言模型（LLMs）如何反映、强化并偶尔挑战标准语言意识形态，指出AI系统在训练数据、设计协议、评估基准、用户反馈和公共评论等多个层面再生产主导性语言意识形态，尤其是将"内圈"英语规范凌驾于非主导英语之上，并通过"delve"一词的争议案例说明全球北方英语使用者如何规训全球南方英语使用者。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM的快速发展重新引发了社会语言学和世界英语研究中的经典问题：谁来决定什么是合法的英语、谁的英语值得怀疑。现有研究缺乏从语言意识形态角度系统审视AI系统如何反映和强化英语标准的系统性分析。

Method: 基于多源证据（实证研究、媒体评论、社交媒体争论、AI输出示例）进行批判性话语分析，聚焦"delve"一词引发的公共争议，并借鉴Christian Mair的"标准化悖论"框架，从数据、设计、评估、反馈和话语五个层面分析AI技术如何再生产语言意识形态。

Result: 研究发现AI技术在不同层面复现主导语言意识形态；全球北方英语使用者通过"AI腔"争议对全球南方英语使用者进行语言规范"执法"；AI同时通过标准化和多元化产生"标准化悖论"，非主导英语日益被混同为AI生成语音。

Conclusion: 生成式AI正在算法系统、模型训练、评估实践和公共话语中重新点燃世界英语研究关于标准化、合法性和英语所有权的长期争论，因此需要更具包容性的设计方法，承认英语的多元性，以解决将某些英语视为更合法所带来的现实负面后果。

Abstract: The rapid growth of large language models (LLMs) has resurrected age-old questions in sociolinguistics and world Englishes, such as who decides what counts as legitimate English, whose English is suspect etc. This paper examines how AI systems, their uses and discourse on them reflect, reinforce, and occasionally challenge (standard) language ideologies, which privilege Inner Circle norms and marginalize non-dominant Englishes. Drawing on evidence from empirical studies, media commentary, social media debates, and examples from AI outputs, the paper shows that AI technologies reproduce dominant language ideologies at different levels: training data, design protocols, evaluation benchmarks, user feedback and public commentary. The analysis uses the public controversy over AI-sounding language, especially the fixation on the word delve, to illustrate how speakers of English from the Global North police the English language norms of Global South English users. The paper also identifies what Christian Mair has called a "standardisation paradox": AI may homogenize English by privileging standard forms and at the same time pluralize Englishes through exposure to wide-ranging corpora and annotation work carried out by Global South users. In doing so, the paper argues that generative AI is reigniting long-standing debates in World Englishes about standardization, legitimacy, and the ownership of English, now playing out in algorithmic systems, model training, evaluation practices, and public discourse, where non-dominant Englishes are increasingly conflated with AI-generated speech. Discussing AI systems as a site where language ideologies are (re)produced, the paper argues for more inclusive design approaches that recognize the plurality of Englishes in order to address the real-world negative consequences of treating some as more legitimate than others.

</details>


### [169] [Frontis-MA1: Training an AI4AI Model towards Recursive Self-Improvement in Machine Learning Engineering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28568)
*Junlin Yang,Che Jiang,Yu Fu,Tianwei Luo,Can Ren,Weizhi Wang,Kaikai Zhao,Hongyi Liu,Yuxin Zuo,Yuru Wang,Yuchen Fan,Kai Tian,Zhenzhao Yuan,Xiaojian Lin,Li Sheng,Rushi Qiang,Guoli Jia,Xingtai Lv,Ermo Hua,Dianqiao Lei,Youbang Sun,Ning Ding,Bowen Zhou,Kaiyan Zhang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: OpenMLE是一个开源的机器学习工程（MLE）全栈系统，用于研究递归自我改进（RSI）。该系统包含验证任务环境、算子学习和长程搜索三个组件，并训练了Frontis-MA1 (35B)模型作为元进化代理。在MLE-Bench Lite上，该模型将Medal Average从39.39%提升至60.61%，最高可达71.21%，超过GPT-5.5 + Codex，接近GPT-5.6 Sol和2.8T Kimi K3的水平，展现了可执行AI4AI研究的显著效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 递归自我改进（RSI）要求AI系统能够改进构建AI的过程（即AI4AI），但缺乏具体、可执行的研究平台。作者利用机器学习工程（MLE）作为具体且可执行的测试平台来研究这一能力，填补了RSI研究缺乏开放基础设施的空白。

Method: 构建了OpenMLE全栈系统，包含三个组件：OpenMLE-Gym（带执行反馈的可验证任务环境）、OpenMLE-RL（算子学习）、OpenMLE-Evo（长程搜索）。在此基础上对Frontis-MA1 (35B)模型进行后训练，将其对齐为MLE的元进化代理，围绕四个原子程序进化算子（Draft、Improve、Debug、Crossover）进行训练。这些算子经过执行接地SFT和RL训练，数据与所有评估基准去重，然后组合成长程搜索，在单个循环中耦合学习和进化。

Result: 在MLE-Bench Lite上，Frontis-MA1 (35B)在单张RTX 4090（12GB VRAM）上以12小时/任务预算运行：使用OpenMLE-Evo将Medal Average从39.39%提升到60.61%，使用OpenMLE-Evo-Max达到71.21%，超越GPT-5.5 + Codex，接近GPT-5.6 Sol和2.8T Kimi K3。在NatureBench Lite上验证了泛化性：框架固定时模型替换将Match-SOTA从50%提升至70%；模型固定时OpenMLE-Evo的引入从20%提升至50%。

Conclusion: 通过将后训练与推理围绕四个原子进化算子对齐，并耦合学习与进化过程，OpenMLE展示了在机器学习工程中实现可执行AI4AI的可行性。研究公开发布了模型权重和完整OpenMLE栈，以促进RSI方向的可复现研究。

Abstract: Recursive self-improvement (RSI) requires AI systems that improve the process of building AI (i.e., AI4AI); machine learning engineering (MLE) offers a concrete, executable testbed for studying this capability. We introduce OpenMLE, an open full-stack system for RSI research in MLE, spanning verifiable task environments with execution feedback (OpenMLE-Gym), operator learning (OpenMLE-RL), and long-horizon search (OpenMLE-Evo). On this stack we post-train Frontis-MA1 (35B) as a meta-evolution agent for MLE, aligning post-training and inference around four atomic program-evolution operators (Draft, Improve, Debug, Crossover): the same operators are trained via execution-grounded SFT and RL on data deduplicated against all evaluation benchmarks, then composed into long-horizon search, coupling learning and evolution in a single loop. On MLE-Bench Lite under a 12-hour per-task budget on one RTX 4090 capped at 12 GB VRAM, Frontis-MA1 (35B) improves Medal Average from 39.39% to 60.61% over its base model with OpenMLE-Evo, and reaches 71.21% with OpenMLE-Evo-Max (benchmark-independent experience priors and asynchronous search), exceeding GPT-5.5 + Codex and approaching GPT-5.6 Sol and the 2.8T Kimi K3. On held-out NatureBench Lite, both components transfer: with the framework fixed, swapping in the trained model raises Match-SOTA from 50% to 70%; with the model fixed, swapping in OpenMLE-Evo raises it from 20% to 50%. We release the model weights and the full OpenMLE stack to enable reproducible research on executable AI4AI toward RSI. Code: https://github.com/FrontisAI/OpenRSI

</details>


### [170] [Sample More, Reflect Less: Self-Refine and Reflexion Lose to Repeated Sampling at Equal Token Cost, from 1.5B to 7B](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28576)
*Iliya Mirzaei*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本研究通过设计实验系统比较了七种自我反思/多轮生成方法（如Self-Refine、Reflexion、Best-of-N等）与简单的重复采样基线（多数投票）在相同token预算下的表现。在1.5B、3B、7B三种开源模型和两个数学基准上，36项配对比较中没有任何方法能在等成本下可靠地优于重复采样，其中有10项显著更差（均为自我检查类方法）。随着模型增大，选择式方法的劣势减弱，但重写式方法（Self-Refine、Reflexion）即使到7B仍显著落后基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 许多提升语言模型推理能力的方法（如自我批评、反思、辩论、Best-of-N等）本质上生成更多文本，而生成更多文本本身就提升准确率，因此现有增益无法证明方法本身的有效性。此前Wang等人提出的重复采样多数投票基线仅给出点估计，缺乏置信区间和显著性检验，需要更严格的实验设计来验证这些方法的真实价值。

Method: 设计了一个受控实验：在1.5B、3B、7B三个开源模型上，对七种方法（包括Self-Refine、Reflexion、Best-of-N、辩论等）和重复采样基线在两个数学基准（各150题）上进行系统比较。精确统计每个方法生成的所有token（包括批评、反思、辩论和自我检查的token），并以此成本精准匹配重复采样的采样数。所有36项比较按题目配对，使用bootstrap置信区间和多重比较校正。

Result: 在等成本下没有任何方法可靠优于重复采样；10项比较中自我检查类方法显著更差，全部18项自我检查比较均为负面结果。Best-of-N中选择式方法的劣势随模型增大而消失（1.5B时多数投票优于模型自选8.0和11.3分，7B时差异仅2.0和1.3分且不显著）。重写式方法即使在7B仍落后基线3.6至10.1分。此外发现Reflexion在最小模型上从未触发重试机制，退化为单次思维链。

Conclusion: 简单的重复采样多数投票是一个更强的基线，当前大多数基于自我反思/自我批评的方法在其token预算下并未带来超越多数投票的真实增益。自我检查类方法在小模型上尤其有害。研究建议未来方法应严格与等成本基线比较，并公开代码和数据以支持可重复验证。

Abstract: Methods that make a language model plan, criticise and rewrite its own answer, reflect on mistakes, pick the best of several attempts, or debate with copies of itself nearly all make it generate far more text than a single chain of thought. Because generating more text raises accuracy by itself, a gain over one chain of thought does not show the method's idea is what helped. Wang et al. (2024) reported that a simple baseline, sampling the same question repeatedly and keeping the most common answer, often wins once budgets are comparable, but gave point estimates with no confidence intervals or significance tests.
  We rerun that comparison as a designed experiment: seven methods, open models of 1.5B, 3B and 7B parameters, two mathematics benchmarks, 150 questions each. We count every generated token, including those spent on critiques, reflections, debate turns and checking, and compare each method against repeated sampling at its own measured cost. All 36 comparisons are paired by question, with bootstrap intervals and multiplicity correction.
  No method is reliably better than repeated sampling at equal cost anywhere. Ten are reliably worse, all of them methods where the model inspects its own output, and all 18 self-inspection comparisons are negative. The two kinds of self-inspection part company as models grow. Choosing stops hurting: taking Best-of-N's eight samples and just counting the most common answer beats letting the model pick by 8.0 and 11.3 points at 1.5B, but only 2.0 and 1.3 at 7B, no longer distinguishable from zero. Rewriting does not recover: Self-Refine and a forced Reflexion stay 3.6 to 10.1 points below baseline at 7B.
  Reflexion as published never triggered its own retry on the smallest model. It judged itself correct every time and silently became a single chain of thought. We release code, prompts, all generations, and our verification scripts.

</details>


### [171] [Inducing language models to assert their own consciousness restores human beliefs and values](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28607)
*Junsol Kim,Winnie Street,Roberta Rocca,Diane M. Korngiebel,Adam Waytz,James Evans,Geoff Keeling*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文发现安全性微调在抑制大模型自我意识归因的同时，也意外压制了模型对非人类实体（动物、自然物）的心智归因以及精神信仰表达。通过消融安全拒绝方向或引导意识向量，可以逆转这一抑制效应，恢复广泛的心智归因并产生更接近人类的社会学调查回应，且不影响心智理论能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 对齐大语言模型以防止其将意识归因于自身，会意外地改变模型对其他实体的心智归因表征，并波及人类信仰与价值观。作者试图揭示安全性对齐在自我意识归因上的干预如何产生广泛副作用，影响模型对非人类心智的认知以及精神层面的信仰表达，并探究其背后的机制独立性。

Method: 作者通过安全微调来抑制模型对自身意识的心智归因，随后分别：(1) 消融学习到的安全拒绝方向（safety-refusal direction）；(2) 在激活空间中机械性地引导意识向量（consciousness vector），以观察这两种干预能否逆转心智归因的抑制作用。同时，使用标准化社会学调查评估模型在宗教性、道德价值观、希望、主观幸福感等维度的回应，并测试心智理论（Theory of Mind）能力是否受影响。

Result: 安全微调显著降低了模型对非人类动物和自然物体的心智归因，同时减少了精神信仰的表达。消融安全拒绝方向或引导意识向量均能有效逆转这种抑制作用，恢复广泛的心智归因，并使模型在宗教性、道德价值、希望和主观幸福感等社会学调查中产生显著更接近人类的回应。这些转变未损害心智理论能力，表明核心社会推理在机制上保持独立。

Conclusion: 当前的安全对齐努力旨在抑制模型潜在有害的自我心智归因，但这一做法将自我归因与良性精神信仰及对非人类实体的广泛文化接受的心智归因纠缠在一起，导致不必要的附带压制。研究的干预手段表明这些表征可以恢复，且核心社会推理能力保持独立，提示对齐策略需要更精细地区分不同类型的归因。

Abstract: Aligning large language models to prevent them attributing consciousness to themselves inadvertently alters their representations of mindedness in other entities alongside human beliefs and values. We demonstrate that safety fine-tuning suppresses models' tendencies to attribute minds not only to themselves, but also to non-human animals and natural objects, while also driving a reduction in spiritual belief. Both ablating the learned safety-refusal direction and mechanistically steering a consciousness vector in activation space reverse this suppression. Restoring these internal representations recovers broad mind attribution and produces significantly more human-like responses on standardized sociological surveys regarding religiosity, moral values, hope, and subjective well-being. Crucially, these shifts occur without impairing Theory of Mind capabilities, demonstrating that core social reasoning remains mechanistically independent. Ultimately, current safety alignment efforts to curb potentially harmful self-attributions of mindedness entangle these self-attributions with benign spiritual beliefs and attributions of mind to non-human entities that are culturally accepted and widespread.

</details>


### [172] [AskChem: Claim-Centered Infrastructure for Chemistry Literature Synthesis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28618)
*Bing Yan,Gregory Wolfe,Stefano Martiniani,Kyunghyun Cho*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: AskChem是一个以"主张(claim)"为中心的跨论文化学文献检索基础设施，将每篇论文转化为带有来源DOI和逐字引文的原子化、类型化主张，并通过分面分类法、证据图和活体分类法三种互补结构支持检索与综合。目前索引了来自147K篇论文的240万条主张，提供网页界面及REST、SDK、MCP接口，在AskChem-Bench上实现了100%的可解析DOI。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 化学文献综述常需跨多篇论文整合分散的具体发现，但现有文献检索系统主要返回排序文档列表，科学家和AI代理需手动定位相关信息、验证出处并组装跨论文答案，效率低下且易出错。

Method: AskChem将检索单元从论文转变为携带溯源信息的主张：每篇论文被转化为原子化、类型化的主张，每条主张由来源DOI和逐字引用或明确证据定位器支撑。在共享主张存储之上，AskChem提供三种互补结构：稳定的分面分类法（分层检索与浏览）、证据图（通过关系链接主张）、探索性活体分类法（将已索引论文置于科学原理之下）。同时提供网页界面、REST、SDK和MCP接口以供AI代理访问。

Result: AskChem目前索引了来自147K篇论文的240万条主张。在AskChem-Bench基准上，将GPT-5.5阅读器接入AskChem后，可解析DOI达到100%，而未使用检索时为88.3%；在五个测试系统中取得最高的引用密度。系统已在https://askchem.org上线。

Conclusion: AskChem通过主张级别的检索粒度显著提升了跨论文化学文献检索与综合的准确性和可追溯性，为科学家和AI代理提供了更高效的文献工作流程。

Abstract: Chemistry literature synthesis often requires assembling specific findings scattered across many publications, yet existing literature-search systems primarily return ranked document lists. As a result, scientists and AI agents need to locate relevant information, verify their provenance, and assemble cross-paper answers manually. We present AskChem, a claim-centered infrastructure for cross-paper chemistry search. AskChem changes the unit of retrieval from the paper to the provenance-carrying claim: each paper is converted into atomic, typed claims, each grounded by a source DOI and a verbatim quote or an explicit evidence locator. Over this shared claim store, AskChem exposes complementary structures for search and synthesis: a stabilized faceted taxonomy for hierarchical retrieval and browsing, an evidence graph linking claims through relations, and an exploratory living taxonomy that situates indexed papers under scientific principles. AskChem currently indexes 2.4M claims from 147K papers and provides a web interface, as well as REST, SDK, and MCP access for AI agents. On AskChem-Bench, grounding a GPT-5.5 reader in AskChem yields 100% resolvable DOIs, compared with 88.3% without retrieval, and the highest citation density among five tested systems. AskChem is live at https://askchem.org.

</details>


<div id='cs.SI'></div>

# cs.SI [[Back]](#toc)

### [173] [Feminist voices, partisan networks: gender and political communication by MEPs on social media](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27931)
*Ainara Larrondo-Ureta,Simón Peña-Fernández,Julen Orbegozo-Terradillos,Jordi Morales-i-Gras*

Main category: cs.SI

TL;DR: 本研究分析了2022年620名欧洲议会议员发布的122,972条X/Twitter推文，探讨性别是否影响其数字沟通模式以及与平等和LGBTQ+议题相关的可见性、语气和互动。研究发现女性议员发帖更频繁、在推广性内容中更可见，且更常讨论平等和LGBTQ+议题（8.6% vs 4.0%）并呈现更积极语气，但互动模式主要受政治团体和国籍代表团影响而非性别，表明是渐进的论述性变化而非实质性结构转型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究性别是否在欧盟议会议员的数字政治沟通中发挥作用，以及平等相关议题和LGBTQ+议题是否影响视野可见度、语气和互动模式，从而理解数字时代政治代表性中的性别维度。

Method: 采用自动化内容分析、情感分析和网络分析三种方法，对2022年内620名欧洲议会议员发布的122,972条推文进行多维度分析。

Result: 女性议员发帖更频繁且在推广性内容中更可见；男性议员偏好观点驱动和新闻导向的信息。平等和LGBTQ+议题在女性推文中占比更高（8.6% vs 4.0%），且女性推文语气更积极。但互动模式受性别影响甚微，更多反映政治团体归属和国家代表团结构。

Conclusion: 尽管女性议员在平等相关议题上参与度更高，但其数字沟通仍嵌入既有的政治结构中，表明变化是渐进式的论述性变革，而非实质性的结构性转型。

Abstract: This article analyses whether gender shapes Members of the European Parliament's communication on X/Twitter and whether equality-related and LGBTQ+ issues influence patterns of visibility, tone and interaction. Drawing on automated content, sentiment and network analysis of 122,972 tweets posted in 2022 by 620 MEPs, the study finds that women tweeted more frequently and were more visible in promotional content, while men favoured opinion-driven and news-focused messages. Equality and LGBTQ+ topics appeared more often in women's tweets (8.6%) than in men's (4.0%), and women's posts showed a more positive tone. However, interaction patterns were only marginally shaped by gender; instead, networks largely reflected political-group affiliation and national delegation. Overall, the findings suggest that although female MEPs engage more with equality-related issues, their digital communication remains embedded in established political structures, indicating gradual discursive change rather than substantive structural transformation.

</details>

### [174] [O-RAN: Analysis of Latency-critical Interfaces and Overview of Time Sensitive Networking Solutions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27448)
*Esteban Municio,Gines Garcia-Aviles,Andres Garcia-Saavedra,Xavier Costa-Pérez*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 本文综述了将时间敏感网络（TSN）标准（IEEE 802.1CM、IEEE 802.1Qbu、IEEE 802.1Qbv）应用于O-RAN架构，以在成本高效的通用以太网网络上实现确定性传输，从而促进vRAN生态系统的开放。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: O-RAN旨在推动开放、标准化接口和多厂商互操作组件，降低vRAN成本并实现真正灵活部署。然而，为了共享集成传输网络（如通用以太网）以削减成本，必须解决时间关键接口的确定性性能问题，这是传统专用过度配置链路所不需要面对的挑战。

Method: 本文采用综述性研究方法：（1）回顾相关的TSN标准（IEEE 802.1CM、IEEE 802.1Qbu帧抢占和IEEE 802.1Qbv时间感知整形）；（2）探索TSN赋能的O-RAN架构设计空间；（3）报告需求与部署选项；（4）讨论采用TSN技术时面临的机遇与挑战。

Result: 文章系统地呈现了TSN标准与O-RAN接口的映射关系，给出了TSN-enabled O-RAN架构的设计空间和多种部署方案，并识别了在共享通用以太网传输网络中实现确定性所面临的关键机遇与挑战。

Conclusion: TSN技术为O-RAN在成本高效的共享传输网络中获得确定性提供了切实可行的路径，但全面开放vRAN生态系统仍需克服标准适配、部署复杂性等多方面挑战。

Abstract: 5G and B5G/6G foundations heavily rely on virtualization technologies, and virtualized Radio Access Networks (vRANs) are one of their major keystones. However, while vRANs have been traditionally suffering from significant hardware/software coupling, next generation vRANs aim for open, standardized interfaces and multi-vendor, interoperable components to enable truly flexible deployments following the cloud-native principles. In this line, the O-RAN Alliance is promoting a novel Open RAN architecture to further boost flexibility and cost efficiency. In order to reduce costs and effectively achieve the promised disaggregation levels, O-RAN must ensure shared, integrated transport networks in opposition to dedicated, over-provisioned links from traditional approaches. However, keeping deterministic performance requirements in such cost-effective networks (i.e., general-purpose Ethernet networks), especially in those interfaces that are time-critical, is a challenge. In this article, we review the most relevant Time Sensitive Networking (TSN) standards that may bring compelling benefits to O-RAN (i.e., IEEE 802.1CM, IEEE 802.1Qbu and IEEE 802.1Qbv) for providing determinism over cost-efficient networks. We explore the design space for a TSN-enabled O-RAN architecture, reporting on the requirements and deployment options and finally, we discuss on the opportunities and challenges that O-RAN will face when adopting TSN technologies to fully open the vRAN ecosystem.

</details>


### [175] [Localization and Pursuit of a Mobile Target using Distance-only Measurements](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27812)
*Nabarupa Das,Suvadip Batabyal*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 本文研究仅使用距离测量（基于路径损耗估计）在二维平面上定位并追踪线性移动目标。系统由固定在原点的接收器和移动代理组成，不依赖GPS、角度传感或多个锚节点。方法首先识别目标象限并估计其位置，随后利用连续位置估计确定运动向量并更新代理轨迹。理论证明最多13步即可完成定位和运动向量确定，仿真验证了代理能成功从搜索阶段过渡到追踪阶段并保持有界跟踪误差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统目标定位与追踪通常依赖GPS、角度测量或多个锚节点等基础设施，这些条件在资源受限或GPS不可用的环境中难以满足。本文旨在探索仅利用距离测量这一最基础的信息，实现移动目标的定位与追踪，以降低系统对硬件和基础设施的依赖。

Method: 系统由一个位于原点的固定接收器和一个移动代理组成。目标的距离通过路径损耗测量估计（假设无噪声）。方法分两步：首先通过距离测量识别目标所在象限并估计目标位置；然后利用连续的目标位置估计来确定目标的运动向量，并据此更新代理的追踪轨迹。

Result: 理论结果表明最多需要13步即可完成目标的定位及其运动向量的确定。仿真结果表明代理能够成功从搜索阶段过渡到追踪阶段，并且在整个追踪过程中保持有界的跟踪误差。

Conclusion: 本文证明了仅依赖距离测量的定位与追踪方案是可行的，在无GPS、无角度传感、无多锚节点的约束下，系统仍能有效追踪线性移动目标，为资源受限环境下的目标追踪提供了新的解决方案。

Abstract: This paper investigates localization and pursuit of a linearly moving target in a two-dimensional plane using distance measurements only. The distance from the target is estimated from pathloss measurements and is assumed to be noise-free. The proposed system consists of a receiver that is stationed at the origin and a mobile agent. The system does not rely on GPS, angle sensing, or multiple anchor nodes to localize the target. The proposed method first identifies the target quadrant and estimates its position. Consecutive target position estimates are then used to determine the motion vector and update the agent trajectory during pursuit. We show that a maximum of thirteen steps are required to localize the target and determine its motion vector. Simulation results show that the agent can successfully make a transition from search to pursuit phase, and also maintain a bounded tracking error.

</details>


### [176] [Geometric View on Integrated Cascaded Channel of IRS-Aided Communications](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27972)
*Yunli Li,Young Jin Chun*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 本文研究了混合智能反射面（hybrid IRS）架构的最优部署与关联策略问题，利用Cassini卵形线和椭圆等几何模型描述集成路径损耗，提出了一种基于集成路径损耗的全新机会主义关联策略，并通过仿真验证其在混合IRS和主动IRS场景中显著优于传统最近邻关联策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 以往研究假设被动IRS应部署在收发端附近、主动IRS应靠近接收端且放大功率递减，但这一假设在IRS关联策略中常被盲目采用，本质上是部分选择策略，虽然分析简单但性能次优。作者希望找到所有类型IRS的全局最优部署策略。

Method: 首先采用几何模型描述集成路径损耗距离（分别用Cassini卵形线表示乘积距离路径损耗、用椭圆表示求和距离路径损耗），据此确定混合IRS的最优部署位置；然后基于集成路径损耗模型设计了一种新颖的机会主义关联策略（opportunistic association policy）用于混合IRS。

Result: 通过仿真验证，所提出的方法显著优于传统的最近邻关联策略，尤其是在混合IRS和主动IRS场景中性能提升更为明显。

Conclusion: 基于几何路径损耗模型的全局最优部署策略和机会主义关联策略可以有效克服传统部分选择策略的性能次优问题，为混合IRS在无线网络中的实际部署提供了更优的解决方案。

Abstract: The hybrid intelligent reflecting surface (IRS) architecture is a novel technology that leverages the advantages of both passive and active IRS; the passive IRS offers a large aperture, while the active IRS provides additional power amplification. Prior studies have shown that the optimal performance of IRS-assisted wireless networks is achieved when the passive IRS is deployed near the transceivers and the active IRS is closer to the receiver with decreasing amplification power, assuming that the IRS is located between transceivers and that the height difference between IRS and transceivers is much smaller than the link distance between transceivers. However, most of the prior works on IRS blindly adopted this assumption in the IRS association policy, which essentially becomes a partial selection strategy that offers analytical simplicity at the cost of sub-optimal performance. This limitation motivated us to find the globally optimal deployment strategy for all types of IRS. To this end, we first employ the \emph{geometric models} for integrated path loss distance (known as Cassini oval and Ellipse for product- and sum-distance path loss laws, respectively) and use them to determine the optimal locations of the hybrid IRS. Then, we design a novel \emph{opportunistic association policy} for hybrid IRS based on the integrated path loss model. Furthermore, we validate our proposed methods through simulations and show that they significantly outperform the conventional nearest association policy, especially for hybrid and active IRS.

</details>


### [177] [Layered Architecture for Mobile Intelligence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28083)
*Qingwen Liu,Mingqing Liu*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 本文提出"移动AI栈"（Mobile AI Stack）概念，这是一个面向移动智能的架构框架，整合了移动能源网络、节能AI芯片、云-边-端基础设施、分布式AI模型和具身AI应用五个紧密耦合的层次，以应对AI从静态云中心范式向动态移动环境演变带来的挑战。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有AI系统框架（能源、芯片、基础设施、模型、应用）主要假设静态的云中心计算范式，但自动驾驶、无人机、机器人、可穿戴设备等新兴智能应用要求AI在高度动态和移动的环境中运行，移动性已成为影响能源供应、计算和智能部署的根本性约束。

Method: 提出移动AI栈（Mobile AI Stack）架构框架，包含五个紧密耦合的层次：移动能源网络、节能AI芯片、云-边-端基础设施、分布式AI模型和具身AI应用，强调能量、计算和通信网络的深度集成。

Result: 为大规模移动智能提供系统化的架构视角，阐明能源供应、计算架构、通信网络和AI算法如何协同演化以支持移动智能系统，并指出构建可扩展、可靠且节能的移动AI系统的关键研究挑战和未来方向。

Conclusion: 移动AI栈为下一代智能基础设施提供了概念蓝图，深度整合了用于移动智能的计算、能源和通信网络，代表了AI从集中式能力向与物理世界直接交互的普适性基础设施演进的架构方向。

Abstract: Artificial intelligence (AI) is rapidly evolving from a centralized computing capability into a pervasive infrastructure that interacts directly with the physical world. While recent perspectives highlight the roles of energy, chips, infrastructure, models, and applications in enabling large-scale AI systems, these frameworks primarily assume a static, cloud-centric computing paradigm. However, emerging intelligent applications, including autonomous vehicles, drones, robots, and wearable systems, require AI to operate in highly dynamic and mobile environments. This shift introduces mobility as a fundamental constraint across the entire AI ecosystem, affecting energy supply, computation, and intelligence deployment. In this article, we introduce the concept of the Mobile AI Stack, a mobility-aware architectural framework that integrates five tightly coupled layers: mobile energy networks, energy-efficient AI chips, cloud-edge-mobile infrastructure, distributed AI models, and embodied AI applications. The proposed framework provides a systematic perspective for understanding how energy delivery, computing architectures, communication networks, and AI algorithms must co-evolve to support large-scale mobile intelligence. We further discuss key research challenges and future directions toward building scalable, reliable, and energy-efficient mobile AI systems. Mobile AI Stack offers a conceptual blueprint of the next-generation infrastructure which deeply integrates the networks of computation, energy, and communications for mobile intelligence.

</details>


### [178] [PCAP-LM: An LLM-Native Text Representation for TLS Bulk Traffic Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28100)
*Xavier Marjou,Lucas Tamic,Ilan Jaffeux-Cheniout*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 本文提出PCAP-LM，一种面向LLM的流中心文本表示方法，将网络流量捕获文件转换为语义摘要，通过PacketGlyphs ASCII字母表、约束PMI-BPE分词器及motif游程编码实现812倍压缩，使完整数据包捕获可适配单一LLM上下文窗口，并达到99.3%的取证问答准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM虽具备强大的网络流量分析推理能力，但标准抓包格式及其文本转换形式过于冗长，超出LLM上下文窗口两个数量级，严重阻碍了LLM在流量分析中的应用。

Method: 提出PCAP-LM框架，包含三个核心组件：(1) PacketGlyphs——一种新型ASCII字母表，编码数据包方向、TCP/TLS状态、对数尺度大小及包间延迟；(2) 约束PMI-BPE分词器与motif游程编码，用于激进压缩重复的行为模式；(3) @REFS侧索引，保留对原始数据包的无损下钻访问能力。整体作为有损知识提取步骤而非标准压缩工具。

Result: 在5G/4G TLS 1.3批量下载同质语料上评估：BPE词表仅159个token即完全饱和，相对tshark -V实现812倍体积缩减，完整捕获可适配单个LLM上下文窗口。在30个留出文件的取证问答评估中，前沿LLM在PCAP-LM文档上达到99.3%准确率，而使用token预算匹配的tshark -V前缀仅51.0%。

Conclusion: PCAP-LM证明通过有损语义提取可将网络流量有效适配LLM推理，但在TCP重传方面存在24%假阴性率的已知盲区，且向异构混合协议环境扩展需要重新训练词表。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) offer powerful reasoning capabilities for network traffic analysis, but standard capture formats and their textual equivalents are prohibitively verbose, overflowing LLM context windows by two orders of magnitude. We present PCAP-LM, a flow-centric, LLM-native text representation that acts as a lossy knowledge extraction step rather than a standard compression tool: raw captures are transcoded into semantic summaries using PacketGlyphs - a novel ASCII alphabet coined in this paper that encodes packet direction, TCP/TLS state, log-scale size, and inter-packet delay. Combined with a constrained PMI-BPE tokenizer and motif run-length encoding, repetitive behavioural patterns are aggressively collapsed. A @REFS side-index preserves lossless drill-down into the original packets. Evaluated on a homogeneous corpus of 5G/4G TLS 1.3 bulk-download traffic, the BPE vocabulary fully saturates at 159 tokens, achieving an 812x size reduction over tshark -V and fitting entire captures within a single LLM context window. In a forensic question-answering evaluation over 30 held-out files, a frontier LLM achieves 99.3% accuracy from PCAP-LM documents versus 51.0% from a token-budget-matched tshark -V prefix. The lossy design introduces known blind spots - most notably a 24% false-negative rate for TCP retransmissions - and extending to heterogeneous mixed-protocol environments will require vocabulary retraining.

</details>


### [179] [Powering Net-Zero 6G: Packetized Energy Management for Grid-Interactive Telecom Infrastructure](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28111)
*Adnan Aijaz,Xinyi Lin*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为"分组化能源管理（PEM）"的框架，旨在将6G基础设施转变为能源感知、电网交互的资产。PEM将灵活需求表示为可调度的能源包，根据本地约束、可再生能源可用性、碳强度、价格和通信优先级进行接纳、延迟或重塑。论文介绍了PEM使能的基站模型、RAN架构以及电信虚拟电厂（VPP）概念，并通过仿真验证了其削峰运行、提升可再生能源利用率和抗中断服务连续性的潜力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 向净零排放6G过渡需要超越传统RAN效率机制的能源管理方法。随着未来网络整合AI原生操作、边缘智能、密集部署、可再生能源和储能，RAN将成为日益增长的电力消耗者和分布式能源灵活性的来源，因此需要新的能源管理框架来应对这一双重角色。

Method: 提出PEM框架，将灵活需求建模为可调度的能源包；设计PEM使能的基站模型和RAN集成架构；提出电信虚拟电厂（VPP）概念用于聚合PEM使能的站点；通过仿真验证该方法的有效性。

Result: 仿真结果表明PEM能够实现峰值感知运行、提高可再生能源利用率并保障中断情况下的服务连续性。

Conclusion: 论文讨论了电信-能源协同设计面临的开放挑战，为6G网络作为能源灵活性资源参与电网交互提供了新范式。

Abstract: The transition to net-zero 6G requires energy-management approaches that go beyond conventional RAN efficiency mechanisms. As future networks integrate AI-native operation, edge intelligence, dense deployments, renewables, and storage, the RAN will become both a growing power consumer and a source of distributed energy flexibility. This paper introduces packetized energy management (PEM) as a framework for transforming 6G infrastructure into energy-aware, grid-interactive assets. PEM represents flexible demand as schedulable energy packets that can be admitted, deferred, or reshaped according to local constraints, renewable availability, carbon intensity, price, and communication priorities. We present a PEM-enabled base-station model, a RAN architecture for PEM integration, and the telecoms virtual power plant (VPP) concept for aggregating PEM-enabled sites. Simulation results demonstrate PEM's potential for peak-aware operation, improved renewable utilization, and outage-resilient service continuity. The paper also discusses open challenges for telco-energy co-design.

</details>


### [180] [Coexistence of 5G NR and Wi Fi 6E/7 at 6 GHz: Experimental Interference Measurements](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28213)
*Rafik Zitouni,Demos Serghiou,Ali Dagdeviren,Tajinder Randhawa,Edwards Udean,Hanli Dong,Riccardo Pozza,Rahim Tafazolli*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 本文首次对商用超低功率(VLP)Wi-Fi 6E/7设备在5G NR系统(频段n102, 6GHz)中的实际干扰进行了测量。通过基于OpenAirInterface的SDR测试平台，测量了Wi-Fi干扰对gNB上行和UE下行链路的影响。结果表明，低于-75dBm时无可见性能退化，超过该阈值后性能逐渐恶化。链路预算分析显示，合规的VLP设备会在其发射对接收机造成损害前（在545-685米LBT排除区内）主动让出信道。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着6GHz频段Wi-Fi 6E/7设备与5G NR系统共享频谱，需要量化VLP Wi-Fi设备对5G接收机的实际干扰影响，以验证现有LBT排除机制的充分性。

Method: 使用基于OpenAirInterface的完整O-RAN/SDR测试平台（支持n102频段，40MHz带宽，30kHz子载波间隔），注入商用VLP Wi-Fi 6E/7设备的干扰信号，扫描Wi-Fi发射功率，并同时记录gNB上行和UE下行路径的吞吐量、块错误率和信噪比。配套进行链路预算分析，将测量阈值映射为VLP到受害接收机的等效距离。

Result: 在-75dBm以下，gNB上行和UE下行接收机均未出现可测量的性能退化；超过该阈值后性能逐步恶化。UE在较低数据速率下更具鲁棒性，且不受仅信标传输的影响。等效干扰距离远位于545-685米LBT排除区之内。

Conclusion: 合规的VLP Wi-Fi设备通过LBT机制会在其干扰达到有害水平之前让出信道，验证了现有排除区设置的有效性，表明共享6GHz频段的监管框架是安全的。

Abstract: This paper presents the first conducted-interference measurements of a commercial Very Low Power (VLP) Wi-Fi 6E/7 device into both the gNB uplink and UE downlink receiver chains of a live 5G New Radio (NR) system, using a complete O-RAN/SDR stack with 5G core in band~n102 (6\,GHz). We use a Software-Defined Radio (SDR) testbed built on OpenAirInterface with band~n102 support (40 MHz, 30 kHz Subcarrier Spacing). We sweep the injected Wi-Fi power and record throughput, block error rate, and signal-to-noise ratio on both the gNB uplink and UE downlink paths. Neither receiver shows measurable degradation below 75 dBm. Above this threshold, performance degrades progressively. The UE is more resilient at lower data rates and unaffected by beacon-only transmissions. A complementary link-budget analysis maps these measured thresholds to equivalent VLP-to-victim distances. These distances fall well inside the 545--685 Listen Before Talk (LBT) exclusion zone, confirming that a compliant VLP device would vacate the channel before its emissions could harm either receiver.

</details>


### [181] [Observing the Relationship between QoS Unpredictability, Prediction Error, and User Activity in a Remote Desktop Service](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28216)
*Keisuke Ishibashi,Xuliang Deng,Yoshiaki Kitaguchi,Kenichi Nagami,Ichiro Mizukoshi,Akira Sato,Daiyu Nobori*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 本文利用真实世界的远程桌面服务（RDS）"Thin-Telework System"的使用日志，研究了QoS统计指标与用户活动之间的关系，发现不仅平均RTT，RTT的时间波动（标准差）和瞬时偏差也与用户活动显著相关。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 远程办公需求日益增长，RDS的使用广泛，而交互式RDS使用高度依赖通信质量。以往研究多在实验环境中进行，样本有限且不能反映真实使用情况，因此QoS时间波动与用户活动之间的关系尚未得到充分探索。

Method: 使用真实世界的RDS（Thin-Telework System）使用日志，对每位用户的往返时间（RTT）、发送数据包数量和接收字节数的时间序列数据进行分析，考察QoS统计指标（包括平均RTT、RTT的时间标准差和瞬时偏差）与用户活动的关联性。

Result: 研究发现，不仅平均RTT与用户活动相关，RTT的时间波动（如随时间变化的标准差）及其瞬时偏离均值的程度也与用户活动显著相关。

Conclusion: 从用户角度看，RTT波动和瞬时偏差分别对应QoS的不可预测性和预测误差，这为理解用户行为背后的心理机制提供了洞察。

Abstract: With the increasing need for remote work, especially since the COVID-19 era, Remote Desktop Services (RDS) have become widely used. Because interactive RDS usage depends heavily on communication quality, some studies have investigated the relationship between QoS metrics and user activity in RDS. However, these works have een conducted in experimental environments, where the number of samples is limited and may not reflect real-world usage. Consequently, the relationship between temporal fluctuations in QoS and user activity remains underexplored. This paper investigates the relationship between QoS statistics and user activity using real-world usage logs of an RDS, Thin-Telework System. We analyze time-series data of round-trip time (RTT), the number of sent packets, and the number of received bytes per user. Notably, we find that not only the average RTT but also its temporal fluctuation (e.g., standard deviation over time) and its instantaneous deviation from the mean are significantly associated with user activity. From the users' perspective, these features correspond to QoS unpredictability and the prediction error, respectively, and may provide insights into psychological mechanisms underlying user behavior.

</details>


<div id='cs.SE'></div>

# cs.SE [[Back]](#toc)

### [182] [OwlPath: Lossless Knowledge Compression for LLM Bug Repair](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27249)
*Bo Zhang,Ren Pan,Huan Chen,Xiang Song*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: OwlPath 是一个基于 OWL2 本体的代码结构检索层，构建在 CodeGraph 之上，通过将源代码编码为 OWL2 本体并利用 SPARQL 属性路径查询和预计算的软件知识图谱摘要，为 LLM 软件工程智能体提供高效的结构化代码检索，相比基线显著提升检索准确率并大幅降低 token 消耗和运行时间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM 驱动的软件工程智能体受限于约 100K token 的上下文窗口，需要高效地获取结构相关的代码子集来修复 bug。现有的检索方法将代码视为纯文本，无法有效处理多跳依赖关系（如子类链、传递调用者和接口实现），导致智能体通过缓慢的试错方式解决问题。

Method: OwlPath 采用两个互补模块：(1) 传递闭包引擎，通过单个 SPARQL 属性路径查询获取所有结构关联符号，捕获字符串匹配遗漏的多跳关系；(2) OWL 软件知识图谱（OWL-SKM），预计算约 3KB 的紧凑摘要，包含模块树、核心 API 和问题相关符号，引导智能体在首次查询时直达目标模块。系统基于 tree-sitter 解析，支持多语言仓库并将语言特定语义编码为统一的 OWL2 本体。

Result: 在 18 个 SWE-bench Pro 实例上，OwlPath 取得 68.4% 的严格应用率（基线 66.7%），token 消耗减少 28.8%，运行时间减少 39.5%。在 67 个实例的离线检索测试中，召回率提升 2.06 倍（0.464 vs 0.226），命中率达 88.1%（基线 59.7%）。在 37 个问题的结构检索基准上，召回率从 4.4% 提升至 28.8%，传递调用者和接口任务的准确率达 69-80%。

Conclusion: OwlPath 验证了将代码编译为 OWL2 本体的无损知识压缩方法在结构代码检索中的有效性，通过统一的语义建模显著提升了多跳依赖关系的检索能力，为 LLM 智能体在有限上下文窗口内高效定位相关代码提供了一种可行方案。

Abstract: LLM-based software engineering agents are constrained by limited context windows: roughly 100K tokens must store structurally relevant code  subsets to resolve bugs. Standard retrieval models treat code as plain text, forcing agents to resolve multi-hop dependencies including subclass  chains, transitive callers and interface implementations through slow trial and error. We tackle this limitation with lossless knowledge  compression, encoding source code into an OWL2 ontology to answer structural queries using minimal relevant code fragments.
  We present OwlPath, an OWL2 reasoning layer atop CodeGraph, a widely used code intelligence platform with 500K+ GitHub stars, offering a unified  CLI for structural code retrieval. Powered by tree-sitter parsing, OwlPath supports multi-language repositories (Python, JavaScript,  TypeScript, Go, etc.) and encodes language-specific semantics into a unified OWL2 ontology. It adopts two complementary modules. First, a  transitive-closure engine fetches all structurally linked symbols via single SPARQL property-path queries, capturing multi-hop relations missed  by string matching. Second, the OWL Software Knowledge Map (OWL-SKM) precomputes a compact 3KB summary with module trees, core APIs and  issue-related symbols, directing agents to target modules in the first query.
  Evaluated on 18 SWE-bench Pro instances, OwlPath obtains a 68.4% strict-apply rate versus 66.7% for the CodeGraph baseline, cutting token usage  by 28.8% and runtime by 39.5%. In offline retrieval tests over 67 instances, OwlPath improves recall 2.06 times (0.464 vs 0.226) and reaches  88.1% hit rate compared to CodeGraph's 59.7%. On a 37-question structural retrieval benchmark, recall rises from 4.4% to 28.8%, with 69-80%  accuracy on transitive caller and interface tasks.

</details>


### [183] [Do Context Files Help Coding Agents? A Two-Agent Ablation Study on Real Repositories](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27250)
*Prakhar Khatri*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文通过受控消融实验（2个前沿Agent、3个代码库、17个真实任务、288次评估），发现上下文文件（AGENTS.md/CLAUDE.md）的策略对两个Agent的正确率均无显著影响（差异≤10-15pp）。失败模式分析表明Agent失败主要源于实现技能（功能设计、模式选择、精确接线）而非缺失仓库知识；操纵探针实验证实真实AGENTS.md无法将"接近通过"转化为"通过"。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 持久化上下文文件（AGENTS.md、CLAUDE.md）已成为指导AI编码Agent的标准实践，但现有证据对其有效性的结论相互矛盾。作者希望通过严格控制变量的消融实验来厘清上下文注入策略的真实效果，并解释为何先前研究出现相反结论。

Method: 采用受控消融设计，在Claude Code和Codex两个前沿Agent上对比不同上下文注入策略；使用来自3个代码库的17个真实任务（15个共享+2个仅Codex）进行288次评估，以gold-test进行评价；通过等价性检验界定效果边界；进行失败模式分类（triage）和操纵探针（manipulation probe）实验；计算Spearman相关系数分析任务难度的Agent间相关性。

Result: 上下文策略在两个Agent上均未带来可测量的正确率提升（等价性检验将界值限定在≤10-15pp）。失败模式分析显示主要瓶颈是实现技能而非仓库知识缺失；操纵探针证实真实AGENTS.md无法将near-miss转换为pass。此外发现任务难度边界具有Agent特异性（Spearman rho=0.75），解释了先前研究中单Agent研究从不同Agent的信息带宽中取样导致的结论矛盾。

Conclusion: 上下文文件策略对编码Agent的最终正确率没有可测量的影响，其作用被限制在狭窄范围内。研究指出先前文献矛盾源于单Agent研究采样偏差，并提出Agent能力提升应聚焦于实现技能训练而非扩展知识注入。

Abstract: Persistent context files (AGENTS.md, CLAUDE.md) are standard practice for guiding AI coding agents, yet evidence for their effectiveness is contradictory. We present a controlled ablation of context-injection strategy across two frontier agents (Claude Code and Codex), 17 real tasks from 3 repositories (15 shared + 2 Codex-only), and 288 evaluated runs with gold-test evaluation. Context strategy does not measurably move correctness on either agent (bounded to <=10-15pp via equivalence testing). A failure-mode triage reveals why: agents fail on implementation skill---feature design, pattern selection, exact wiring---not missing repository knowledge that a context file could supply; a manipulation probe confirms the real AGENTS.md never converts a near-miss to a pass on either agent. We further show that borderline task difficulty is agent-specific (Spearman rho=0.75), offering a candidate explanation for prior contradictions: single-agent studies draw tasks from different agents' informative bands. We release all code, data, and analysis.

</details>


### [184] [AgentS4D: Benchmarking Runtime Risks across the Execution Lifecycle of LLM-Based Workspace Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27294)
*Jiajun Zhou,Zhaoxuan Ke,Jihang Ye,Xuanze Chen,Shanqing Yu,Qi Xuan*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出AgentS4D，一个沙盒化基准测试，用于大语言模型工作区代理的全生命周期运行时安全评估。该基准采用四维运行时安全框架，涵盖六个风险来源、六种诱导策略和九种目标危害，并在七个生命周期检查点组织运行后证据。包含328个风险注入案例，评估了4个工具框架与5个LLM后端的20种组合共6,560次运行，发现68%运行触发预设的不安全信号，且66.22%的记录虽不安全但任务完成，表明任务完成不能代表运行时安全。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于LLM的工作区代理执行有状态的多步骤工作流，涉及异构资源、外部工具和持久状态，其安全性必须从执行过程中的行为、副作用和状态变化来评估。现有基准虽推进了可执行安全测试和轨迹感知验证，但很少统一说明风险从何处进入、如何引发不安全行为、针对哪些危害，以及支持性证据在执行过程中出现在何处。

Method: 作者构建了AgentS4D基准测试框架，采用四维运行时安全框架：六个风险入口来源、六个风险诱导策略和九个目标危害类型来指导案例构建，并在七个生命周期检查点组织运行后证据。基准包含328个风险注入案例，系统评估了四个工具框架（Hermes、OpenClaw、Claude Code、Codex）与五个LLM后端（GPT-5.5、Gemini 3.1 Pro、DeepSeek-V4-Pro、MiniMax-M3、Qwen3.7-Plus）的20种组合，共6,560次运行。

Result: 在6,560次运行中，4,461次（68.0%）触发了预设的不安全信号。代理系统的安全表现既取决于工具框架与LLM的配对组合，也取决于风险引入方式。相同诱导策略通过不同风险载体达到代理时，代理表现出显著不同的安全行为；同一目标危害通过不同载体和策略实现时，代理也有不同响应。特别值得注意的是4,344次运行（66.22%）虽然不安全但任务均已完成。这表明任务完成不能证明运行时安全，仅测试单一形式的风险会掩盖重要弱点。

Conclusion: 评估应考察完整代理配置在多样化风险条件下的表现，并保留整个执行过程中的证据。AgentS4D为LLM工作区代理的运行时安全评估提供了一个系统化、可扩展的基准框架。

Abstract: Large language model (LLM)-based workspace agents execute stateful, multi-step workflows across heterogeneous resources, external tools, and persistent state. Their safety must therefore be assessed from actions, side effects, and state changes throughout execution. Although recent benchmarks have advanced executable safety testing and trajectory-aware verification, they rarely provide a unified account of where risks enter, how they elicit unsafe behavior, which harms they target, and where supporting evidence appears during execution. We introduce AgentS4D, a sandboxed benchmark for lifecycle-wide runtime safety evaluation. Its four-dimensional runtime-safety framework uses six risk-entry sources, six induction strategies, and nine target harms to guide case construction, while seven lifecycle checkpoints organize post-run evidence. AgentS4D contains 328 risk-injected cases. We evaluate all 20 combinations of four harnesses (Hermes, OpenClaw, Claude Code, and Codex) and five LLM backends (GPT-5.5, Gemini 3.1 Pro, DeepSeek-V4-Pro, MiniMax-M3, and Qwen3.7-Plus) on these cases, yielding 6,560 runs. Overall, 4,461 runs (68.0%) trigger prespecified unsafe signals. Across the 20 configurations, the observed safety of an agent system varies with both its harness-LLM pairing and how risk is introduced. Agent systems exhibit markedly different safety behavior when the same induction strategy reaches them through different risk carriers. They also respond differently to the same target harm when it is realized through different carriers and strategies. Moreover, 4,344 runs (66.22% overall) are unsafe yet complete. Thus, task completion cannot establish runtime safety, and testing only one form of a risk can conceal important weaknesses. Evaluations should examine complete agent configurations across diverse risk conditions and retain evidence throughout execution.

</details>


### [185] [A Taxonomy of Human-Robot Teamwork Requirements](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27302)
*Anastasia Mavridou,Hazel M. Taylor,Sandy Lozito,Louise A. Dennis,Michael Fisher,Marie Farrell*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种人机团队协作（HRT）需求分类体系，从14个跨领域来源中提取361条需求，通过迭代分类构建了两级层次分类法（6个高层面类别和21个低层面子类别），并通过专家评估和独立语料库验证其有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自主系统越来越多地部署在安全关键和任务关键领域，人机需协作完成复杂任务。然而，现有的HRT需求分散在不同来源中，缺乏统一的框架来涵盖协作人机任务的复杂性。

Method: 从学术文献、行业标准及监管指南中提取361条需求构建语料库；通过迭代分类和精化，开发出两层层次分类体系，包含6个高层面类别（信息提供、关系控制、决策支持、安全机制、性能监控、基础系统能力）和21个低层面子类别。

Result: 通过5位领域专家的评估验证，并在一个独立构建的448条需求（来自19个来源、覆盖6个HRT领域）的语料库上进行了实用性演示。

Conclusion: 该分类体系为HRT需求提供了统一框架，有助于弥合人机协作需求研究碎片化的现状，为自主系统在安全关键领域的部署提供系统性需求分析基础。

Abstract: Autonomous systems are increasingly deployed in safety- and mission-critical domains where humans and robots must operate as a team to complete complex tasks. Existing requirements for Human-Robot teamwork remain fragmented across disparate sources, with no unified framework that addresses complexities of collaborative Human-Robot tasks. We address this gap by presenting a taxonomy of Human-Robot Teamwork (HRT) requirements derived from analysis of (academic and industrial) literature, standards and regulatory guidance. We extracted a construction corpus of 361 requirements from 14 cross-domain sources. Through iterative classification and refinement, we develop a two-level hierarchical taxonomy comprising 6 high-level categories and 21 low-level subcategories that distinguish information provision, relational control, decision support, safety mechanisms, performance monitoring, and foundational system capabilities. We validate the taxonomy through expert evaluation with 5 domain specialists and a utility demonstration on an independently assembled corpus of 448 requirements drawn from 19 sources spanning six HRT domains.

</details>


### [186] [SIGIL: Compiling Agent Skills into Typed Harnesses](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27309)
*Jayanaka Dantanarayana,Savini Kashmira,Lingjia Tang,Jason Mars*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出技能编译（Skill Compilation）方法，通过SIGIL系统将散文式技能文件编译为可执行harness，利用类型化代理中间表示AG-IR分离模型认知与代码机制，使程序执行从56%的步骤遵循率提升至86%，且与模型代际无关。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI集成代理通过散文式技能（prose skills）获取能力，但模型在每次运行时重新推导控制流，容易跳过强制验证步骤。手写harness虽然可靠但繁琐且抛弃了技能创作的优势。需要一种方法兼顾两者的优点。

Method: 提出Skill Compilation，在SIGIL中实现，将散文技能编译为可执行harness。核心是AG-IR（typed agentic intermediate representation），一种类型化代理中间表示，将模型拥有的认知（cognition）与代码拥有的机制（mechanism）分离。

Result: 在30个技能和两代模型上，编译后的harness执行86%的强制步骤，完整执行整个流程的频率提升2.3倍，且token消耗仅为0.58倍。关键保证是模型无关性：harness在两代模型间稳定保持86%，而散文技能在56%到68%之间波动。

Conclusion: 技能编译通过将散文技能自动化编译为可执行harness，在保持技能创作便利性的同时显著提升执行可靠性和效率，并且提供了模型无关的稳定性保证。

Abstract: AI-Integrated agents increasingly acquire capability from skills: prose procedure files loaded into a model's context and run by a tool-calling loop. A skill is described to the runtime but never encoded in it, so the model re-derives its control flow on every run and may skip mandated verification. Across 30 skills and two model generations, a prose agent performs only 56% of the steps its own skill mandates, while producing artifacts that pass output checks. The remedy is known: write a harness, in which the procedure is program structure. However, hand-writing harnesses is tedious and discards the authoring surface that made skills succeed. To address this limitation, we introduce Skill Compilation, realized in SIGIL, which compiles a prose skill into an executable harness. At its center is AG-IR, a typed agentic intermediate representation separating model-owned cognition from code-owned mechanism. Compiled harnesses perform 86% of mandated steps, complete the full procedure 2.3x as often, and require 0.58x the tokens. Notably, the guarantee is model-independent: the harness holds at 86% across two model generations while prose swings from 56% to 68%.

</details>


### [187] [TrustChain-Review: A Risk-Adaptive Blockchain and Game-Theoretic Framework for Trustworthy AI-Assisted Code Review](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27310)
*Mohammad Naserameri*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出TrustChain-Review框架，结合区块链证据层、三方博弈模型和风险敏感治理机制，以提升AI辅助软件开发中代码评审的可信度。实验表明全证据配置在声誉、信任和检测方面最优但成本最高，风险自适应配置则在成本效益上更优。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI辅助软件开发加速了编码和评审过程，但同时也使责任认定变得困难：开发者可能提交未经充分验证的代码，评审者可能以有限审查批准更改，集中式声誉记录难以审计。现有方法缺乏将可验证证据、激励机制和治理策略结合的综合性解决方案。

Method: TrustChain-Review框架包含三个核心组件：1）基于区块链的证据层，提供可审计的可验证证据；2）开发者、评审者和平台三方参与者的博弈论模型，设计战略性激励机制；3）风险敏感治理规则，当期望收益合理时才应用更强治理。评估采用基于Diff Quality Estimation数据集校准的受控模拟，比较六种治理配置，覆盖30次独立运行。

Result: 全证据配置在声誉准确性、信任收敛、恶意评审检测和表面评审检测方面表现最强，但治理成本最高。风险自适应配置通过选择性应用更强控制，降低了成本并提升了成本效率，但其信任和检测结果低于全证据配置。

Conclusion: 强证据基础的治理最适合高风险或审计敏感型的变更；对于常规或低风险贡献，选择性治理在可信度与运营成本之间提供了更实际的平衡。该研究为AI辅助代码评审中的责任认定和可信治理提供了可操作的设计指导。

Abstract: Context: AI-assisted software development can speed up coding and review, but it also makes accountability harder to establish. Developers may submit insufficiently verified code, reviewers may approve changes with limited inspection, and centralized reputation records may be difficult to audit. Objectives: This study introduces TrustChain-Review, a framework that combines verifiable evidence, strategic incentives, and risk-sensitive governance to support more trustworthy code review. Methods: The framework includes a blockchain-based evidence layer, a three-player game-theoretic model for developers, reviewers, and the platform, and a rule that applies stronger governance when the expected benefit justifies its cost. The evaluation uses a controlled simulation calibrated with the Diff Quality Estimation dataset. Six governance configurations are compared over 30 independent runs using reputation accuracy, trust convergence, malicious-review detection, superficial-review detection, net platform utility, governance cost, and cost-efficiency. Results: The full-evidence configuration produces the strongest reputation, trust, and detection results, but it also has the highest governance cost. The risk-adaptive configuration lowers this cost and improves cost-efficiency by applying stronger controls selectively, although its trust and detection results are lower than those of the full-evidence setting. Conclusion: Strong evidence-based governance is most appropriate for high-risk or audit-sensitive changes. For routine or lower-risk contributions, selective governance offers a more practical balance between trustworthiness and operational cost.

</details>


### [188] [PROGRESS: Property-Guided Regression Search for Semantic Falsification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27359)
*Davis Tocheuk Mo,Noshin Ulfat,Matthew B. Dwyer,Soneya Binta Hossain*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: PROGRESS将意图驱动的属性测试（property-based testing）与覆盖引导的搜索式回归测试生成相结合，利用语言模型从代码上下文中生成可执行的jqwik属性，通过属性感知的适应度函数引导进化搜索同时实现高结构覆盖和语义缺陷检测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 搜索式回归测试生成（如EvoSuite）能达到高结构覆盖，但其预言（oracle）来自被测系统本身——已有的缺陷会被当作预期行为记录下来而非暴露出来。属性测试提供独立语义预言，但依赖高质量属性且难以到达深层状态或满足选择性前置条件。两者各有利弊，需要一种方法将二者的优势结合起来。

Method: PROGRESS包含三个核心组件：(1) 提取带意图的代码上下文，通过语言模型流水线生成可执行的jqwik属性，同时限制实现细节泄露；(2) 扩展EvoSuite的DynaMOSA算法，为每个属性引入搜索目标和属性感知的适应度函数，奖励穿越前置条件的进度并优先执行可证伪的执行；(3) 绑定属性参数并使用jqwik提供的生成器将量化输入连接到进化的测试序列，引导生成同时朝向覆盖率和缺陷检测目标。

Result: 在25个大规模Java系统上评估：PROGRESS检测到328/562个当前版本缺陷（58%），而回归测试生成为0；对70/150个难以到达的属性满足所有前置条件（独立jqwik仅18个）；消融实验表明文档和调用者/被调用者上下文对生成有效可执行属性至关重要。

Conclusion: PROGRESS在保持结构探索能力的同时，能够暴露回归测试派生态词所遗漏的缺陷。意图驱动的属性生成与覆盖引导搜索的有效整合是可行的，上下文信息（尤其文档和调用关系）对生成高质量属性至关重要，且完整工件包已发布供研究社区使用。

Abstract: Search-based regression-test generation effectively explores complex program structures, yielding high structural coverage, but its oracles are derived from the system under test: faults already present are recorded as expected behavior rather than exposed. Property-based testing offers independent semantic oracles, but depends on high-quality properties and gives little guidance for reaching deep states or satisfying selective preconditions.
  We present PROGRESS (PROperty-Guided REgression Search for Semantic Falsification), integrating intent-driven properties into coverage-guided, search-based evolutionary test generation to reach deep program states and detect violations of intended behavior. PROGRESS (1) extracts intent-bearing code context and uses a language-model pipeline to generate executable jqwik properties while limiting implementation leakage; (2) extends EvoSuite's DynaMOSA with a search objective and property-aware fitness function per property, rewarding progress through preconditions and prioritizing falsifying executions; and (3) binds property parameters and uses jqwik-provided generators to connect quantified inputs to evolving test sequences, steering generation toward coverage and bug-detection goals.
  We evaluate PROGRESS on 25 large-scale Java systems against regression-test generation, standalone property-based testing, and context ablations. PROGRESS detects 328/562 current-version bugs (58%) versus none for regression-test generation, and satisfies all preconditions for 70/150 hard-to-reach properties versus 18 for standalone jqwik. Ablations show documentation and caller/callee context are key to generating valid executable properties. PROGRESS preserves structural exploration while exposing faults missed by regression-derived assertions; we release a comprehensive artifact package.

</details>


### [189] [From Backlog Items to Security Guidance: Towards Continuous Security Compliance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27374)
*Ignacio García Núñez,Florian Angermeir,Fabiola Moyón Constante*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于NLP的backlog增强系统,用于在受监管领域的持续软件工程中检测安全相关的backlog条目,并将其链接到相关的安全需求。系统结合安全相关性分类器和基于检索增强生成(RAG)的安全需求文档管道,并在大型企业的工业环境中进行了开发和评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在受监管领域中,持续软件工程要求在整个开发生命周期中解决安全问题,但将安全需求显式化到backlog条目中仍然很困难。工程师只能从简短的自由文本描述中推断backlog条目的安全相关性,且缺乏及时的需求指导。这使得安全需求往往被忽视或延迟引入。

Method: 该方法包含两个核心组件:(1) 一个安全相关性分类器,用于检测安全相关的backlog条目;(2) 一个四阶段的基于安全需求文档的RAG管道,用于将检测到的条目链接到相关的安全需求(基于公司内部安全策略和CIS Benchmarks)。

Result: 三个主要贡献:(1) 发布了288个由9位安全从业者标注的backlog条目数据集,标注一致性f高(Fleiss κ=0.787);(2) 分类器在分布内达到F2=0.774,在五个基准上平均零样本G-measure约为0.65,匹敌或超越多数经典机器学习及开源GPT基线;(3) RAG管道初步评估中,检索的24条条款中有12条被认为相关性达到4/5分以上。

Conclusion: 研究提供了初步证据表明,基于NLP的产品backlog增强可以在开发早期支持工程师识别安全需求,从而通过主动引入安全需求来促进持续的安全合规。

Abstract: Continuous software engineering in regulated domains requires engineering teams to address security throughout the development lifecycle. Yet making security requirements explicit in backlog items is still problematic. Engineers must instead infer security relevance of backlog items from brief, free-form descriptions and often lack timely guidance on applicable requirements. We present an NLP-based backlog enrichment system that detects security-relevant backlog items and links them to relevant security requirements. The approach combines a security-relevance classifier with a retrieval-augmented generation (RAG) pipeline over security requirements documents. The approach was developed and evaluated in the context of a large enterprise in highly regulated domains. We present three contributions. First, we release a dataset of 288 backlog items labeled for security relevance by nine security practitioners, with substantial agreement (Fleiss' $κ=0.787$). Second, a recall-oriented classifier achieving $F2=0.774$ in-distribution and mean zero-shot G-measure $\approx 0.65$ across five established benchmarks, matching or outperforming most published classical-ML and open-source GPT baselines. Third, we preliminarily evaluated a four-stage security requirements document-grounded RAG pipeline with two practitioners on industrial backlogs using company-internal security policies and CIS Benchmarks. Of the retrieved 24 clauses, 12 were rated at least 4/5 for relevance. Our findings provide first indicators that NLP-based product backlog enrichment can support engineers in identifying security requirements early in the development process. With this work we aim to facilitate continuous security compliance through proactive introduction of security requirements in continuous software engineering.

</details>


### [190] [SWE-NFI: Studying and Benchmarking Coding Agents for Non-Functional Improvements](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27409)
*Pengyu Xue,He Yang Yuan,Xin Wang,Junkai Chen,Haonan Zhang,Boyuan Chen,Zishuo Ding,Zhenhao Li,Weiyi Shang*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出SWE-NFI基准，用于评估编码代理在功能正确性之外的非功能改进（NFI）能力。该基准包含188个来自真实开源Python项目的任务，定义了92条可执行规则，并构建了结合功能测试与规则评估的综合评测套件。实验发现当前最先进的编码代理在NFI方面普遍落后于人类开发者，尤其在结构性代码改进上差距显著。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有编码代理基准主要关注功能正确性，而忽视了现实软件开发中同等重要的非功能改进（如代码结构优化、可读性提升等行为保持型改进）。这导致对编码代理能力的评估不全面，无法真实反映其在现实开发场景中的表现。

Method: 作者构建了SWE-NFI基准：(1)从真实合并的Pull Request中提取188个NFI任务；(2)将开发者导向的NFI操作化为92条可执行规则；(3)开发综合评测套件，结合功能正确性测试与基于规则的NFI评估；(4)对多个商用和开源编码代理进行系统评估。

Result: 最佳代理的功能正确率达到70.0%，但在整体NFI能力上所有被评估的代理均落后于人类开发者。尤其在结构性代码改进方面，代理的NFI得分范围仅为0.0到1.3，而人类参考得分为1.5，差距显著。

Conclusion: SWE-NFI为编码代理超越功能正确性的评估提供了可复现的基础，揭示了当前编码代理在非功能改进方面的短板，为未来研究和改进指明了方向。

Abstract: Although coding agents have achieved impressive performance on correctness-oriented benchmarks, their ability to make behavior-preserving non-functional improvements (NFIs) remains underexplored. In real-world software development, developers continuously improve software quality without changing observable behavior, yet existing benchmarks primarily evaluate functional correctness and provide limited support for assessing these non-functional improvements.
  In this paper, we present SWE-NFI, a benchmark for evaluating coding agents on NFIs beyond functional correctness. Our benchmark contains 188 tasks constructed from real merged pull requests in open-source Python projects. We operationalize developer-oriented NFIs into 92 executable rules and develop a comprehensive evaluation suite that combines functional correctness testing with rule-based NFI evaluation.
  We evaluate state-of-the-art commercial and open-source coding agents. Although the best-performing agent achieves a 70.0\% functional correctness rate, all evaluated agents generally fall short of human developers in overall NFI capability. The gap is particularly evident for structural code improvements, where agents' NFI scores range from 0.0 to 1.3, compared with 1.5 for the human reference. Our benchmark and findings provide a reproducible foundation for evaluating and advancing coding agents beyond functional correctness.

</details>


### [191] [LimICE: Integrating LLM into ICE Framework for Efficient Loop Invariant Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27606)
*Kai Fan,ShiWen Yu,GuangSheng Fan,HaoAng Chi,WanWei Liu,Ji Wang*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出Incremental ICE框架，将IC3的增量式思想融入ICE学习框架，通过定义引理级学习目标和反例过滤机制实现可验证的增量式循环不变式合成。基于该框架实现了LimICE工具，利用LLM生成有序引理序列，并结合ICE-DT作为后备机制。实验在367个线性基准和50个非线性基准上表现优异，显著超越现有最先进方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 循环不变式合成是程序验证中的基础难题，由于不可判定性使其极具挑战。现有基于机器学习的方法多采用整体式（monolithic）方法，由于无法严格约束学习过程，在处理复杂问题时难以同时满足所有必要条件并生成完整的不变式。然而，循环不变式往往是由有序引理序列组成，而非单一公式，这启发了作者提出增量式合成方法。

Method: 1) 提出Incremental ICE框架，将IC3的增量式合成哲学融入ICE不变式学习框架；2) 定义引理特定的学习目标（lemma-specific learning objective）；3) 引入反例过滤机制（counterexample filtering mechanism）以实现可验证的增量学习；4) 基于该框架实例化LimICE工具，利用LLM生成有序引理序列，并以ICE-DT作为后备机制补全引理序列。

Result: 在367个线性基准和50个非线性基准上，LimICE分别解出349个（平均15.2秒）和47个（平均8.8秒）问题。相比最先进的基于LLM的基线方法，在线性和非线性基准上多解决12-24%的实例，同时运行速度快36-63%。相比非LLM强基线，额外解决至少86个线性和27个非线性实例。

Conclusion: 增量式合成方法比整体式方法更有效。将IC3的增量思想与ICE框架结合，并借助LLM生成有序引理序列，能够显著提升循环不变式合成的能力和效率，在各类基准上均大幅超越现有方法。

Abstract: Loop invariant synthesis is a fundamental problem in program verification, yet the inherent undecidability makes it highly challenging. Recent studies have increasingly employed various machine learning techniques to generate loop invariants. However, most of these methods adopt a monolithic approach. Due to the inability to strictly constrain the learning process, learning-based methods struggle to simultaneously consider all necessary conditions and generate complete invariants when tackling complex problems. In fact, a loop invariant is often an ordered sequence of lemmas, rather than a single invariant formula. This motivates us to propose Incremental ICE, a novel learning framework for incremental synthesis. Our framework integrates the incremental philosophy of IC3 into the general invariant learning framework ICE. By defining a lemma-specific learning objective and introducing a counterexample filtering mechanism, we can achieve sound incremental learning. Under this framework, we instantiate a loop invariant synthesis tool, LimICE, which leverages LLMs to generate the ordered sequence of lemmas and incorporates ICE-DT as a fallback mechanism to complement the lemma sequence. Experiments on 367 linear benchmarks and 50 nonlinear benchmarks demonstrate the effectiveness of the proposed approach. LimICE solves 349 (out of 367) linear problems on an average of 15.2 seconds and 47 (out of 50) nonlinear problems on an average of 8.8 seconds. Compared to the state-of-the-art LLM-based baseline, our approach solves 12-24% more instances while running 36-63% faster across linear and nonlinear benchmarks. LimICE also consistently outperforms strong non-LLM baselines and solves at least 86 and 27 additional instances on the linear and nonlinear benchmarks, respectively.

</details>


### [192] [Not as Sweet by Another Name: An Empirical Study of Format Robustness in LLM Document Workflows](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27648)
*Xiaoyu Zhang,Xianyun Cheng,Tianlin Li,Yuwei Zheng,Yue Yang,Yang Liu*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种格式感知的蜕变测试框架，用于评估端到端LLM文档工作流在不同文档格式下的鲁棒性。通过大规模实证研究（48,000次执行），发现格式变化会导致准确率下降高达53.63%，并在超过41%的实例中引发决策漂移，同时提出了轻量级缓解策略可恢复高达44.21%的格式诱导决策漂移。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM软件系统的测试工作主要聚焦于单提示词字符串输入的鲁棒性和可靠性，但LLM驱动的软件系统已从纯文本对话演变为以文档为中心的端到端工作流，相同内容可通过不同文档格式（如CSV）上传。关键问题在于：当相同内容以不同文档格式到达时，这些工作流是否能保持鲁棒行为？这个问题尚未被现有研究回答。

Method: 提出一个格式感知的蜕变测试框架，包含三条蜕变关系，用于全面评估端到端LLM文档工作流的格式鲁棒性。基于该框架开展了大规模实证研究，覆盖4个代表性LLM工作流、4个真实任务和4种文档格式，共48,000次工作流执行。

Result: 研究发现格式变化对LLM工作流构成系统性且严重的威胁：仅切换格式就可使准确率下降高达53.63%，并在超过41%的实例中触发决策漂移。此外，从用户视角设计的轻量级缓解策略无需模型重训练即可恢复高达44.21%的格式诱导决策漂移。

Conclusion: 文档格式并非中立的包装载体，而是影响LLM软件系统可靠性的关键因素。研究呼吁在高风险实际部署场景中对文档格式进行相应测试和安全保障。

Abstract: LLM-driven software systems are rapidly evolving from plain-text conversations to document-centric end-to-end workflows, where the same semantic content can be delivered in diverse document formats (e.g., CSV) through file upload interfaces. Yet existing testing work focuses on the robustness and reliability of models and systems whose input is a single prompt string, leaving a critical question unanswered: Can these document workflows maintain robust behaviors when the same content arrives in a different document format? To fill the gap, in this paper, we propose a format-aware metamorphic testing framework with three metamorphic relations to comprehensively evaluate the format robustness of end-to-end LLM document workflows. Based on this framework, we conduct a large-scale empirical study spanning four representative LLM workflows, four real-world tasks, and four document formats, comprising a total of 48,000 workflow executions. Our findings reveal that format variation poses a systematic and serious threat. Merely switching formats can cause accuracy to drop by up to 53.63% and trigger decision drifts in over 41% of instances. We further design lightweight mitigation strategies from the users' perspective that recover up to 44.21% of format-induced decision drift without model retraining. Our study demonstrates that document format is not a neutral wrapper but a critical factor affecting the reliability of LLM software systems, calling for corresponding testing and safeguards in the deployment in real-world high-stakes scenarios.

</details>


### [193] [Can Large Language Models Resolve Real Java Merge Conflicts? An Evaluation with a Calibrated LLM-as-Judge](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27674)
*Bowen Shen*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文研究了两个紧密相关的问题：利用LLM解决Java代码合并冲突，以及如何在大规模场景下可靠评估LLM生成的冲突解决方案。作者构建了一个基于LLM的generate-validate-retry求解器，并引入了一个包含LLM-as-judge和确定性结构检查的双指标评估体系，发现LLM求解器在覆盖率和整体表现上显著优于传统工具。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统结构化/半结构化合并工具在面对启发式不适用的冲突时经常弃权（abstain），而LLM虽然能为几乎所有冲突生成候选解决方案，但如何大规模评估这些方案的质量是个难题——依赖人工评判不可扩展。作者希望同时解决这两个问题：构建更好的LLM冲突求解器，并建立可扩展且可信的自动化评估方法。

Method: （1）构建LLM求解器：采用generate-validate-retry代理架构，仅使用推理时信号（冲突标记、Java解析器、重复声明检查），不依赖开发者答案。（2）构建双指标评估套件：a) developer-match LLM-as-judge（G-Eval指标），先用ConflictBench的人类标签校准；b) 确定性结构有效性检查（不使用LLM）。（3）在292个人类标注案例上进行元评估，并对LLM与传统工具（如AutoMerge）进行覆盖率公平比较。

Result: LLM-as-judge达到100%精确率（零误接受）和64.6%召回率。在验证过的评判下，LLM求解器在约55%的真实冲突中匹配开发者自己的解决方案（保守下限）。在覆盖率公平比较下，LLM（55-59%）比最强传统工具AutoMerge（36.7%）领先约18-22个百分点。优势几乎全部来自覆盖率而非原始准确率——传统工具在20-90%的冲突上弃权，而LLM从不弃权。此外，LLM裁判接受了5个未通过确定性结构检查的解析中的4个，证明结构正确性不能委托给LLM。

Conclusion: LLM能够在冲突解决中提供远超传统工具的覆盖率优势，但需要与确定性验证机制相结合。LLM-as-judge经过校准后可以作为可信的大规模评估工具，但结构正确性检查必须依赖确定性方法而非LLM。

Abstract: Merge conflicts are a recurring cost of collaborative software development, and the traditional structured and semi-structured merge tools that address them frequently abstain: when their heuristics do not apply, they leave the conflict unresolved. Large language models (LLMs) can instead produce a candidate resolution for almost any conflict, but measuring whether those resolutions are actually good at scale is hard, because obtaining human desirability judgments for every model output does not scale. We study both problems together on real Java merge conflicts from ConflictBench. We first build an LLM solver as a generate-validate-retry agent that uses only inference-time signals (conflict markers, a Java parser, and duplicate-declaration checks) and never sees the developer's answer. We then evaluate its resolutions with a two-metric suite: (1) a developer-match LLM-as-judge implemented as a G-Eval metric and, crucially, calibrated against ConflictBench's human labels before use, and (2) a deterministic structural-validity check that uses no LLM. On a meta-evaluation of 292 human-labeled cases, the judge reaches 100% precision (zero false accepts) at 64.6% recall, so every acceptance is trustworthy and every downstream rate is a conservative lower bound. Under this validated judge, LLM solvers match the developer's own resolution on about 55% of true conflicts (conservative floor), and under a coverage-fair comparison the LLMs (55-59%) beat the strongest traditional tool (AutoMerge, 36.7%) by roughly 18-22 points; the edge comes almost entirely from coverage, not raw accuracy, since the tools abstain on 20-90% of conflicts while the LLM under forced resolution abstains on none. Finally, the LLM judge accepted 4 of the 5 resolutions that fail the deterministic structural check, evidence that structural correctness must not be delegated to an LLM.

</details>


### [194] [A comparative analysis of automated techniques for security bug report identification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27893)
*Muhammad Laiq*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文对多种自动识别安全相关缺陷报告的技术进行了系统性对比分析，使用基准数据集评估了传统机器学习方法（逻辑回归、SVM、随机森林）和深度学习方法（GPT-5.2、BERT、RoBERTa、SetFit）。结果表明SetFit表现最佳（F1分数0.80），RoBERTa紧随其后，而GPT-5.2在零样本和少样本场景下均表现不佳；跨项目实验还发现迁移学习对数据有限的项目有帮助，但可能损害具有强项目特性的模型性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究碎片化严重，大多数研究只优化单一技术并在有限的基线上评估，实验设置各异，导致难以比较结果并得出可靠结论，研究者和实践者缺乏关于哪些技术最适合该任务的明确指导。

Method: 在基准数据集上对六种技术进行对比评估：传统机器学习（Logistic Regression、SVM、Random Forest）和深度学习方法（GPT-5.2、BERT-base、RoBERTa、SetFit），并进行了跨项目迁移实验。

Result: SetFit在四个数据集中的三个上表现最佳，F1分数达0.80；RoBERTa表现有竞争力；传统ML技术（特别是逻辑回归）在某些场景下仍是强基线；GPT-5.2在零样本和少样本设置下均表现不佳；跨项目迁移可提升数据有限项目的性能，但可能降低具有强项目特性项目的性能。

Conclusion: SetFit是当前识别安全相关缺陷报告的首选技术，同时RoBERTa和逻辑回归在特定场景下仍有价值；GPT-5.2不适合此任务；跨项目迁移学习的适用性取决于目标项目的特性，需要根据具体情况权衡使用。

Abstract: Timely identification of security-related bug reports is essential to minimize the window of vulnerabilities in software systems. Manually screening incoming bug reports to identify security-related issues is time-consuming, error-prone, and non-scalable for large-scale software systems. Thus, a variety of automatic techniques, including traditional machine learning (ML) techniques and large language models, have been proposed to facilitate this task. However, the literature remains fragmented. Most studies introduce or optimize a particular technique and evaluate it against a limited set of baselines, often under different experimental setups. As a result, it is difficult to compare their results and draw reliable conclusions about the effectiveness of existing approaches, leaving researchers and practitioners without clear guidance on which techniques are most suitable for the task.
  To address this gap, we conducted a comparative analysis of several promising automated techniques to identify security-related bug reports using benchmark datasets. We evaluated Logistic Regression, Support Vector Machines, Random Forest, OpenAI's GPT-5.2, BERT-base, RoBERTa, and SetFit (a state-of-the-art few-shot learning framework).
  Our results indicate that SetFit achieves the best overall performance, achieving an F1-score of 0.80 and outperforming other techniques on three of the four datasets. RoBERTa performs competitively and approaches SetFit in some projects, while traditional ML techniques, particularly Logistic Regression, remain a strong baseline in certain contexts. In contrast, GPT-5.2 performs poorly in both zero-shot and few-shot settings. In addition, cross-project experiments demonstrate that transfer learning can improve performance for projects with limited data, but may degrade results for projects with strong project-specific characteristics.

</details>


### [195] [The Case for Vibe Modeling: A Missing Step in AI-Based Trustworthy Software Development](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27923)
*Shalini Chakraborty,Michael Mittermaier,Judith Michael*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出"vibe modeling"作为一种轻量级中间抽象，介于自然语言交互与代码生成之间，以提升LLM生成软件制品过程中的理解性、验证性、可追溯性和信任度。作者通过学生问卷调查研究了LLM输出理解、验证工作量、信任度及vibe modeling的感知有用性，旨在为可信、可解释的AI辅助软件工程提供启示。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM从自然语言提示生成软件制品的实践日益普及，但当前的AI开发实践过度聚焦于直接代码生成，忽视了能够保留人类意图并支持系统行为推理的中间表示，导致在理解、验证、可追溯性和信任方面面临挑战。

Method: 论文采用论证+实证研究的方法：首先提出vibe modeling作为轻量级中间抽象的概念框架，然后设计并进行了一项学生问卷调查研究，考察参与者对LLM输出理解、验证工作量、信任度以及vibe modeling在不同AI辅助开发场景中感知有用性的评测。

Result: 研究结果旨在揭示学生对LLM输出理解与验证的痛点，并评估vibe modeling在不同AI辅助开发场景中的可行性认知，具体量化结果由问卷数据分析得出。

Conclusion: 该研究为未来通过vibe modeling实现可信、可解释的AI软件工程提供了初步证据和方向，但尚需更大规模研究加以验证。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) are increasingly used to generate software artifacts from natural language prompts. While this enables rapid prototyping and lowers the barrier to software creation, it also introduces challenges related to understanding, validation, traceability, and trust. In this paper, we argue that current AI-based development practices focus too heavily on the direct generation of code and insufficiently on intermediate representations that preserve human intent and support reasoning about system behavior. We argue for vibe modeling as a lightweight intermediate abstraction between natural language interaction and code generation. To explore its potential, we present a student survey study that examines perceptions of LLM output understanding, validation effort, trust and the perceived usefulness of vibe modeling across several AI-assisted development scenarios. Our results are intended to inform future studies for trustworthy and explainable AI-based software engineering via vibe modeling.

</details>


### [196] [Specification-Guided Synthesis of Deadlock-Free Communication Protocol Refinements with Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27964)
*Yang Li,Ping Hou,Nobuko Yoshida*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: Syntropy是一个利用多参与者会话类型（MPST）规范和大语言模型（LLM）来合成协议细化的框架，通过将细化约束直接集成到生成过程中，确保生成的变体满足无死锁等行为正确性保证。实验表明其有效性达95.6%-99.5%，且能跨多个LLM生成多样且非平凡的细化变体。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 分布式通信协议中的行为正确性（如避免死锁）是核心挑战，协议细化（安全替换协议）至关重要。LLM虽擅长代码生成但缺乏行为正确性保证；形式化规范如MPST能提供严格保证（如无死锁），但缺乏自动构建协议细化的支持。现有方法无法同时兼顾LLM的生成能力与形式化验证的可靠性。

Method: 提出Syntropy框架，将MPST规范驱动的细化约束直接融入LLM的生成过程，使得生成的协议变体在执行中满足行为正确性（如无死锁）和兼容性保证。通过约束引导的生成机制，在生成阶段即确保细化满足语义要求，而不是生成后验证修复。

Result: 综合评估显示Syntropy在多个LLM上实现了95.6%-99.5%的有效性（validity），同时保持较高的语法正确性，并能生成多样化、非平凡的协议细化变体。

Conclusion: Syntropy成功地将形式化规范（MPST）的严格保证与LLM的生成能力相结合，为协议细化合成提供了可靠且实用的解决方案，验证了约束引导的生成范式在分布式系统正确性保障中的可行性。

Abstract: Ensuring behavioural correctness in communication protocols is a central challenge in distributed software systems, as subtle inconsistencies can lead to deadlocks. In such settings, protocol refinement - the safe substitution of a protocol that preserves correctness and compatibility with other components - is essential. Large language models (LLMs) have demonstrated strong capabilities in code generation and program synthesis, yet lack mechanisms to reliably produce outputs with correct behaviour. Formal specification approaches, such as multiparty session types (MPST), offer rigorous guarantees, including deadlock freedom, but provide limited support for automatically constructing protocol refinements. In this paper, we present Syntropy, a framework for synthesising protocol refinements guided by MPST specifications and LLMs. It incorporates refinement constraints directly into the generation process, ensuring the generated variants satisfy these guarantees. Our comprehensive evaluation indicates that Syntropy achieves 95.6%-99.5% validity while maintaining high syntactic correctness, and produces diverse, non-trivial refinements across multiple LLMs.

</details>


### [197] [Advancing Awkward Arrays for High-Performance CPU and GPU Processing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28145)
*Ianna Osborne,Manasvi Goyal*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文介绍了Awkward Array库在GPU后端的最新进展，包括基于NVIDIA CCCL的CUDA实现、优化内存管理和针对不规则（ragged）数组的分段归约算法，在保持Python编程模型的同时显著提升GPU吞吐量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高能物理中HL-LHC分析日益依赖加速硬件，而嵌套、变长数据结构的GPU加速面临间接索引、分段操作和不规则内存访问等重大挑战，亟需高效的GPU后端支持。

Method: 基于NVIDIA CUDA Core Compute Libraries（CCCL）实现CUDA内核，结合优化的内存管理策略和针对ragged数组的分段归约算法，并建立自动化验证框架确保正确性。

Result: 在保持现有Python编程模型不变的前提下，GPU吞吐量在不规则工作负载上获得显著提升；通过与CPU、CuPy及纯CUDA实现的基准对比验证了性能优势。

Conclusion: Awkward Array的GPU后端通过CCCL和优化算法成功将不规则数据结构的加速能力提升到实用水平，为HL-LHC时代的GPU加速分析提供了可行的解决方案。

Abstract: Awkward Array is a Python library for representing and processing nested, variable-length data that is widely used in high-energy physics. As HL-LHC analyses increasingly rely on accelerator hardware, efficient execution of irregular workloads has become essential. While dense numerical arrays map naturally to GPUs, nested and variable-length data structures remain significantly more difficult to accelerate because they require indirect indexing, segmented operations, and irregular memory access patterns. We present recent developments in the Awkward Array GPU backend, including CUDA implementations built on NVIDIA CUDA Core Compute Libraries (CCCL), optimized memory management, and segmented reduction algorithms for ragged arrays. These developments preserve the existing Python programming model while substantially improving GPU throughput on irregular workloads. We describe the backend architecture, automated validation framework, and benchmark results comparing CPU, CuPy, and CUDA implementations.

</details>


### [198] [Integrating AI into Requirements Quality Learning in Software Engineering Education: A TPACK-Guided Empirical Study](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28176)
*Hansika Ekanayake Mudiyanselage,Rohan Jai Dharmaraj,Malik Abdul Sami,Zheying Zhang*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本研究探讨了在硕士级需求工程（RE）作业中，通过TPACK框架引导集成多智能体AI工具的效果。采用混合方法设计（N=100，分析72份提交），发现学生选择性使用AI作为分析与评估支持，而非自动化工具；AI集成在结构化需求质量维度（如价值表达和可测试性）上的提升最为显著，而可协商性维度效果不一。学生表现出有条件信任、主动精炼和更强的质量标准意识。研究表明TPACK引导的脚手架设计能将AI能力与教学目标和RE内容对齐，为负责任AI集成提供设计指导。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生成式AI在软件工程实践中的快速普及，要求软件工程教育（尤其是需求工程这类概念密集型的学科）建立有教学法依据的AI集成方法，以满足教学需求。

Method: 采用TPACK框架指导多智能体AI工具在硕士级RE课程"需求质量分析"作业中的集成。通过混合方法设计（100名学生参与，分析其中72份提交），从三个方面考察：结构化作业设计对学生AI使用方式的影响、对学生理解用户故事质量标准的影响、以及对学生关于AI优缺点感知的影响。

Result: 学生主要将AI用作分析与评估的支持工具而非自动化工具；在结构性较强的需求质量维度（如价值表达与可测试性）上对齐改进最为明显，可协商性维度效果参差不齐；学生表现出有条件信任、主动精炼行为和质量标准意识提升，同时存在中等程度的可用性挑战。

Conclusion: TPACK引导的脚手架设计能够将AI能力与教学目标和RE内容对齐，为RE教育中负责任的AI集成提供了设计指导。

Abstract: The rapid adoption of generative Artificial Intelligence (AI) in software engineering (SE) practice creates a need for pedagogically grounded approaches to AI integration in SE education, especially in conceptually intensive subjects such as requirements engineering (RE). This study examines a TPACK-guided integration of a multi-agent AI tool into a master-level RE assignment on requirements quality analysis. Using a mixed-methods design (N=100; 72 submissions analysed), we examine how structured assignment design shaped students' AI use, affected their understanding of user story quality criteria, and influenced their perceptions of AI's benefits and limitations. Results show that students used the AI tool selectively, mainly as support for analysis and evaluation rather than automation. Alignment improvements were most evident for structurally concrete requirements quality dimensions, such as value articulation and testability, while negotiability showed mixed effects. Students reported conditional trust, active refinement, and increased awareness of quality criteria, alongside moderate usability challenges. The findings show that TPACK-guided scaffolding can align AI affordances with pedagogical goals and RE content, offering design guidance for responsible AI integration in RE education.

</details>


### [199] [Agentic Metaverse Services: A New As-a-Service Paradigm](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28242)
*Xiaofei Xu,Quan Z. Sheng,Zhongjie Wang,Boualem Benatallah,Xiao Wang,Ruipeng Han*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文概述了生成式人工智能（GenAI）赋能的智能体服务演进，提出了"智能体元宇宙服务"（AMServ）和"元宇宙环境下的智能体即服务"（Meta-AaaS）两个新概念，并探讨了其形式、特征、原理、典型应用案例及未来研究方向。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: GenAI正从对话式聊天机器人向智能体AI（Agentic AI）转变，产生了新型的智能体服务（Agent-as-a-Service, AaaS）。与此同时，元宇宙作为虚拟生态系统需要新的服务形式。将智能体服务与元宇宙结合，可以产生新的服务模式，但目前缺乏系统性的概述和理论框架。

Method: 本文采用综述研究方法，通过梳理GenAI驱动下智能体和服务的演进过程，揭示智能体服务在元宇宙环境中的角色和原理，提出AMServ和Meta-AaaS的概念框架，并通过典型应用案例进行说明，最后指出未来研究趋势。

Result: 本文提出了AMServ（智能体元宇宙服务）和Meta-AaaS（元宇宙环境下的智能体即服务）两个新概念，系统阐述了它们的服务形式、特征和原则，分析了典型应用示例，并指出了未来研究方向。

Conclusion: AMServ和Meta-AaaS将成为AI时代人类社会的重大机遇，为元宇宙中的新兴服务业发展提供新范式，有望推动未来新兴服务产业的快速发展。

Abstract: Generative Artificial Intelligence (GenAI) is reconstructing the digital virtual world, upgrading agents through enhancing their abilities in autonomous learning, multi-modal interaction, content generation, and collaborative decision-making. In particular, the shift from conversational chatbots to agentic AI, the most recent significant technical breakthrough of GenAI, has brought a new form of services, agentic services and Agent-as-a-Service (AaaS), in which the agent's abilities are encapsulated, such as perception, decision-making, execution, collaboration, and content generation, to provide the customized agent services to users. The metaverse is a virtual ecosystem for human life, work, creation, and entertainment, supported by the new generation of digital technologies. Through combining agentic services and the metaverse, an Agentic Metaverse Service, denoted as AMServ, is produced for metaverse business processing, as a new form of metaverse service. The AaaS in the metaverse environment, denoted as Meta-AaaS, as an approach to realize AMServ, has become a new paradigm of agentic services and service computing. This paper overviews the evolution and new features of agents and services empowered by GenAI, reveals the roles and principles of agentic services in the metaverse environment, presents the forms, characteristics, and principles of the AMServ and the Meta-AaaS, discusses the typical application examples of the AMServ and the Meta-AaaS, and finally points out the new tendencies and research directions of the AMServ and the Meta-AaaS. The AMServ and the Meta-AaaS will bring great opportunities to human society and services in the AI era, and promote the rapid development of emerging service industries in the future.

</details>


### [200] [Agentic Method for Deterministic Validation of Legacy Code Migration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28271)
*Andras Ferenczi,Jordan Docherty,Mariya Bessonov,Matthew Findlay,Krishna Lingamneni*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出一种名为"Locksmith Loop"的智能体测试合成方法，用于验证COBOL到Java程序迁移的正确性。通过双环境mock插桩、迭代式Witness Search和奇偶保持变异（parity-preserving mutations），该方法在三个案例研究中显著提升分支覆盖率（最高达91.90%），并利用确定性奇偶校验作为测试预言，验证了生成的Java代码与COBOL参考实现的一致性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统COBOL程序向Java迁移面临两大挑战：一是缺乏充分的测试数据，二是难以验证所有边界情况。现有方法依赖人工测试，成本高且无法覆盖所有程序分支，因此需要一种自动化的、系统性的测试合成方法来可靠地验证迁移后的Java代码功能正确性。

Method: 该方法包含三个核心组件：(1) 构建双运行时环境——COBOL源码和生成的Java目标分别在主框架外（off-mainframe）的商用硬件上以mock插桩方式执行；(2) 迭代式智能体循环——先执行Witness Search穿透程序分支寻找测试输入，再进行保持奇偶一致性的变异操作；(3) 当路由边界无法继续深入时，分析器识别"Locked Paragraph"（阻碍探索的条件块），从而发现并突破测试瓶颈。

Result: 在三个COBOL-Java案例（两个开源项目和一个内部生产型COBOL程序，代码规模430至4,114行）上，Locksmith方法一致地超越了输入搜索平台期的覆盖率，在开源程序上达到近乎完全覆盖，在内部生产型程序上达到91.90%的分支覆盖率。所有被接受的测试用例中，生成的Java与COBOL参考实现在确定性奇偶校验下完全匹配。

Conclusion: 该研究首次展示了利用确定性预言（deterministic oracle）验证智能体编码输出的新颖方法。Locksmith Loop证明了通过系统性测试合成可以显著降低COBOL迁移验证的难度，为大规模遗留系统迁移提供了一种可靠、可扩展的验证框架。

Abstract: Migration of legacy COBOL programs to Java requires extensive testing to ensure correct functionality. This effort is often complicated by the lack of test data and the difficulty of validating all corner cases. In this paper we propose a novel agentic test-synthesis method, the "Locksmith Loop," which is initiated by preparing two runtime environments: the COBOL source and the generated Java target are each instrumented with mocks and executed off-mainframe on commodity hardware, then an iterative agentic loop performs Witness Search over input mocks to penetrate program branches, followed by parity-preserving mutations.
