When routing boundaries are reached, an analyzer identifies a Locked Paragraph: a condition preventing deeper exploration. Across three COBOL-Java case studies, spanning two open-source programs and one internal production-like COBOL program and ranging from 430 to 4,114 source lines, Locksmith consistently improved coverage beyond input-search plateaus, reaching nearly complete coverage on the two open-source programs and 91.90% branch coverage on the internal production-like COBOL program. The generated Java matched the COBOL reference under deterministic parity checks in all accepted test cases. Through these findings we demonstrate, to the best of our knowledge, a novel approach for validating agentic coding output using a deterministic oracle.

</details>


### [201] [From Textual Requirements to Microservice Architectures - A Comprehensive Evaluation of LLM-Based Design Synthesis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28307)
*Danyllo Albuquerque,José Renan,Guillermo Rodríguez,Guillermo Rodríguez,Emanuel Dantas,Ademar França,Mirko Perkusich,Kyller Gorgônio,Angelo Perkusich*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本研究探讨了LLM（OpenAI o3）能否仅从自然语言需求中合成微服务架构。通过零样本（ZS）和少样本（FS）提示两种方式在Bookstore和PetClinic两个系统上进行混合方法研究，发现FS提示显著优于ZS，服务识别F1从0.79提升到0.97，通信恢复F1从0.61提升到0.82，专家评估也证实FS输出更模块化、连贯和合理。研究展示了LLM在需求驱动架构设计中的潜力，但结果受限于两个小系统和特定模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有微服务分解方法主要基于代码，限制其在仅有文本需求的设计早期阶段的应用。虽然LLM技术进步显著，但缺乏关于LLM能否仅从自然语言需求生成完整微服务架构（包括服务定义和交互）的经验证据。

Method: 采用混合方法研究：使用OpenAI o3在零样本和少样本提示条件下，对Bookstore和PetClinic两个系统各执行一次。评估包括：(1)与参考架构对比，用精确率、召回率和F1评估服务识别和通信恢复；(2)盲审专家评估正确性、完整性、模块化和合理性，并进行开放反馈综合。

Result: 少样本提示下服务识别一致性更高（F1=0.97 vs ZS的0.79）。通信恢复更具挑战性：ZS产生密集架构但精确率低（F1=0.61），FS将F1提升至0.82并减少不支持的依赖。专家评估证实FS输出更模块化、连贯和合理。

Conclusion: OpenAI o3在示例提示引导下展示了从需求到架构设计的潜力，能够弥合需求工程与架构设计之间的鸿沟。但结果具有模型和上下文特异性，来自两个小系统，不能视为与模型无关的普遍性证明。

Abstract: Microservice architectures have become dominant for modernizing monolithic systems, yet identifying appropriate services remains challenging and largely manual. Existing decomposition approaches are predominantly code-centric, limiting applicability in early design stages where only textual requirements are available. Despite advances in Large Language Models (LLMs), limited empirical evidence exists on their ability to synthesize complete microservice architectures from natural-language requirements, including service definitions and inter-service interactions. This study investigates whether an LLM can bridge requirements engineering and architectural design, generating architectures solely from textual requirements and evaluating structural agreement and perceived quality of results. We conduct a mixed-method study using OpenAI o3 under zero-shot (ZS) and few-shot (FS) prompting across two systems (Bookstore, PetClinic), one execution per system/condition. Architectures are evaluated through (i) comparison with reference architectures using precision, recall, and F1-score for service identification and communication recovery, and (ii) a blinded expert assessment of correctness, completeness, modularity, and plausibility, plus open feedback synthesis. OpenAI o3 identifies services with higher agreement under FS prompting (F1 = 0.79 for ZS versus = 0.97 for FS). Communication recovery is more challenging: ZS produces dense architectures with high recall but low precision (F1 = 0.61), while FS improves agreement, reaching F1 = 0.82 and reducing unsupported dependencies. Expert evaluation corroborates these results, with FS architectures perceived as more modular, coherent, and plausible than ZS outputs. OpenAI o3 shows potential for requirements-driven synthesis when guided by exemplar prompting. Results are model- and context-specific from two small systems, not model-independent proof.

</details>


### [202] [Structural Validation of LLM-Generated Microservice Decompositions Using Source-Code Dependencies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28331)
*Daniel Silva,Renan Alves,Emanuel Dantas Filho,Ademar Sousa Neto,Mirko Perkusich,Danyllo Wagner Albuquerque,Kyller Gorgônio,Angelo Perkusich*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文评估了OpenAI o3为PetClinic和Bookstore系统生成的微服务分解在结构依从性方面的表现，提出了一种基于静态依赖分析的自动化验证流水线，并比较了零样本和少样本提示策略。通过规范化类到服务映射覆盖率后，两种提示策略产生了等效的结构依从性（TPD分别为68.0%和83.3%），表明结构性评估必须显式控制映射覆盖率，否则表面差异可能反映方法论偏差而非真实的架构质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将单体系统分解为微服务是软件现代化中的关键活动。虽然LLM能从文本需求生成语义上合理的分解方案，但尚不清楚这些方案是否保留了源代码中实现的结构依赖关系。需要系统性地评估LLM生成分解的结构依从性。

Method: 提出了一种基于静态依赖分析的自动化验证流水线，使用依赖保留（TPD）和依赖违规（TVD）指标，比较零样本和少样本提示策略。还进行了鲁棒性分析以控制类到服务映射覆盖率的差异。

Result: 经规范化后，两种提示策略产生了等效的结构依从性，TPD值在PetClinic上为68.0%，在Bookstore上为83.3%。提示策略之间的表面差异在控制映射覆盖率后消失，说明这些差异可能源于方法论偏差。

Conclusion: 对LLM生成分解的结构性评估应显式控制映射覆盖率，以避免将方法论偏差误判为架构质量差异。

Abstract: Decomposing monolithic systems into microservices is a key activity in software modernization. Although Large Language Models (LLMs) can generate semantically plausible decompositions from textual requirements, it remains unclear whether these proposals preserve the structural dependencies implemented in the source code. This paper evaluates the structural adherence of microservice decompositions generated by OpenAI o3 for the PetClinic and Bookstore systems. We propose an automated validation pipeline based on static dependency analysis and compare zero-shot and few-shot prompting using dependency preservation (TPD) and dependency violation (TVD) metrics. A robustness analysis was conducted to control for differences in class-to-service mapping coverage. After normalization, both prompting strategies produced equivalent structural adherence, achieving TPD values of 68.0% (PetClinic) and 83.3% (Bookstore). The findings demonstrate that structural evaluations of LLM-generated decompositions should explicitly control for mapping coverage, as apparent differences between prompting strategies may otherwise reflect methodological bias rather than genuine architectural quality.

</details>


### [203] [Demystifying Solana Bots: From GitHub Blueprints to On-Chain Fingerprints](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28424)
*Xiaoye Zheng,Yujing Chen,Minghao Wu,David Lo,Difan Xie,Daoyuan Wu,Xiaohu Yang,Zhiyuan Wan*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文对Solana区块链上的机器人（bot）进行了大规模实证研究，从GitHub收集586个bot仓库和Solana链上200个bot地址（超4400万笔交易）两个互补视角，推导出涵盖15个类别、5大领域的实现导向分类体系，识别出五阶段操作流水线，并揭示了跨交易平台和资产的链上交易行为差异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Solana作为高吞吐、低费用的区块链平台，容易遭受bot垃圾交易和金融剥削，且bot相关交易在去中心化交易所的日交易量超过2.5亿美元（2026年1月）。尽管已有研究涉及系统性能、智能合约安全等，但缺乏对Solana bot实际实现方式及其链上可观察行为指纹的系统性理解。

Method: 采用双视角的研究方法：（i）从GitHub收集586个bot仓库进行实现层面分析；（ii）追踪Solana链上200个bot地址，分析超过4400万笔链上交易。基于此推导实现导向的bot分类学，识别操作流水线和交易行为模式。

Result: 得出了Solana bot的15个类别、5大领域（如交易操作、MEV和链上分析）的实现导向分类体系；识别出实践中bot共享的五阶段操作流水线；发现了Solana bot在不同交易平台和资产上链上交易行为的系统性差异。

Conclusion: 基于研究发现，论文提出了未来研究方向，并为在Solana区块链上构建和运营bot提供了建议，填补了对Solana bot实现与实践理解的空白。

Abstract: Solana is an emerging blockchain platform designed for high throughput and low transaction fees, making it inexpensive to submit transactions at scale and, consequently, increasing exposure to bot spamming and related financial exploitation. Solana bots are typically off-chain software systems that operate in a competitive on-chain execution environment by constructing and submitting transactions, and the bot-related transactions on the decentralized exchanges exceed 250 million dollars in daily trading volume in January 2026. Prior studies on Solana have examined system performance, smart-contract security, and specific on-chain phenomena. However, we still lack a systematic understanding of what Solana bots implement in practice and how these implementations manifest as observable on-chain execution fingerprints. To address this gap, we performed a large-scale empirical study of Solana bots from two complementary views: (i) 586 bot repositories collected from GitHub, and (ii) 200 bot addresses on Solana, with over 44 million on-chain transactions. Our study derives an implementation-grounded taxonomy of Solana bots comprising 15 categories grouped into five domains (e.g., Trading Operations, MEV, and On-chain Analytics), identifies a largely shared five-stage operational pipeline manifested in bot implementations, and uncovers systematic variation in on-chain trading behaviors of Solana bots across diverse trading platforms and assets. Based on our findings, we highlight future research directions, and provide recommendations for building and operating bots on the Solana blockchain.

</details>


### [204] [Emerging Challenges in Threat Modeling for GenAI-Augmented Systems: A View from the Trenches](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28431)
*Nicolás E. Díaz Ferreyra,Manish Mahesh Kumar,Nohemí Villarreal,Pankaj Pantel,Immo Brueggemann,Riccardo Scandariato*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文首次在中小型企业（SME）环境中对GenAI感知的威胁建模方法进行探索性评估。通过快速文献综述筛选出三种方法，并将其系统地应用于一个包含GenAI增强系统的工业案例研究，发现各方法识别的威胁存在差异，且对某些GenAI特有风险类别（如软件供应链和以人为本的安全问题）支持有限，同时报告了从业者对方法可用性和集成度的看法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着生成式人工智能（GenAI）在软件系统中日益普及，传统的威胁建模方法（如STRIDE）已不足以评估GenAI特有的新型风险。然而，现有研究缺乏在中小型企业（SME）环境中对GenAI感知威胁建模方法的系统评估，因此需要探索这些方法在实际工业场景中的有效性和适用性。

Method: 本研究采用以下方法：(1) 进行快速文献综述以筛选相关的GenAI感知威胁建模技术；(2) 从文献中选取三种方法；(3) 将这些方法系统地应用于一个包含GenAI增强系统的工业案例研究；(4) 收集并分析从业者对方法可用性和集成方面的反馈。

Result: 研究结果显示：不同威胁建模方法识别出的威胁存在差异；现有方法对某些GenAI特有风险类别的支持有限，尤其是软件供应链相关的和以人为本的安全问题；同时揭示了从业者对方法在SME开发工作流中的可用性和集成度的看法，包括感知工作量和技术采用挑战。

Conclusion: 现有GenAI感知威胁建模方法在SME环境中存在明显的能力缺口，特别是在软件供应链安全和人类中心安全问题方面支持不足。这些发现为改进面向GenAI系统的威胁建模方法提供了实证依据，也为SME在采用这些方法时提供了实践指导。

Abstract: Threat modeling remains a central task in secure software engineering, as it enables the identification of security issues from system architectures. As Generative Artificial Intelligence (GenAI) becomes increasingly pervasive across software systems, traditional threat modeling methods (e.g., STRIDE) are insufficient to assess emerging GenAI-specific risks. In this work, we present the first results from an exploratory assessment of GenAI-aware threat modeling methods in a Small and Medium Enterprise (SME) setting. For this, we conducted a rapid literature review to select relevant techniques and systematically applied three shortlisted methods to an industrial case study involving a GenAI-augmented system. The results highlight differences in the threats identified by each technique and reveal limited support for certain GenAI-specific risk categories, particularly those related to software supply chains and human-centered security issues. We further report practitioners' perceptions of the usability and integration of these methods in SME development workflows, including their perceived effort and adoption challenges.

</details>


### [205] [CoGate: Confidence-Gated Co-Decoding for Secure Code Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28529)
*Minghao Hu,Lannan Luo,Allen Roush,Phillip Howard*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 提出CoGate方法，基于置信度门控的联合解码（co-decoding）框架，在代码生成时动态控制安全专家模型的影响力，以解决现有co-decoding方法在专家模型不自信（OOD场景）时产生误导的问题。在多个LLM后端和基准上优于现有方法CoSec+，在CWEval上Func-Sec@10最高提升12.6%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型在代码生成时可能生成不安全代码。解码时引导（decoding-time steering）通过组合小型专家模型与目标模型（即co-decoding）来生成更安全的代码。但现有co-decoding方法的接受规则未考虑专家模型的置信度，当安全专家因未见过的模式或OOD上下文而不自信时，其引导会产生误导。

Method: 提出CoGate方法，一种置信度门控的co-decoding方法。核心思想是根据专家模型的置信度来控制其在co-decoding过程中的影响力，即当专家模型置信度高时加强其引导，置信度低时减弱或限制其影响，从而避免不自信的误导性建议。

Result: 在多个LLM后端（CodeGen、DeepSeek-Coder、Qwen-Coder、StarCoder）和多个代码生成基准（HumanEval、security suite、CWEval）上进行评估，CoGate优于现有co-decoding方法CoSec+，在CWEval上Func-Sec@10最高提升12.6%。

Conclusion: 置信度门控机制能够有效提升co-decoding的安全性引导效果，通过动态调整专家模型的影响力来应对OOD场景，是一种更为鲁棒的安全代码生成方案。

Abstract: Large language models are widely used for code generation, but they can also produce insecure programs due to patterns learned from their pretraining data. Decoding-time steering has become an important solution to this problem: a small expert model is combined with the target model at each step to generate more secure code, which is referred to as co-decoding. However, the acceptance rule for existing co-decoding approaches does not consider the expert model's confidence. When the security expert is unconfident due to unseen patterns or out-of-distribution (OOD) contexts, its guidance can therefore be misleading. To address the challenge, we propose CoGate, a confidence-gated co-decoding approach that controls the expert's influence on the co-decoding process based on its confidence. We implement our approach and evaluate it across multiple LLM backends (CodeGen, DeepSeek-Coder, Qwen-Coder, StarCoder) on several code generation benchmarks (HumanEval, security suite, and CWEval). Our approach outperforms existing co-decoding methods (CoSec+) across multiple benchmarks, achieving up to a 12.6% gain of Func-Sec@10 on CWEval.

</details>


### [206] [PAIChecker: Uncovering and Checking PR-Issue Misalignment in SWE-Bench-Like Benchmarks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28587)
*Manyi Wang,Junjielong Xu,Pinjia He*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文系统研究了SWE-bench类基准测试中PR-Issue配对错位问题，发现13.6%的实例存在五类模式的错位，并提出PAIChecker多智能体系统来检测此类错位，在三阶段设计中结合模式识别、跨智能体标签合成和代码级验证，在两个基准上达到约92%的准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: SWE-bench类基准被广泛用于评估LLM的问题解决能力，但其构建过程中PR-Issue配对常因大型代码库的复杂性而产生错位，影响基准可靠性和LLM评估的准确性。现有构建方法缺乏系统性的错位检测机制。

Method: 提出PAIChecker多智能体系统，采用三阶段设计：(1) 特定模式识别；(2) 跨智能体标签合成；(3) 代码级验证。该系统用于检测SWE-bench类基准中PR-Issue的错位问题，涵盖五种错位模式和十一个细粒度场景。

Result: 在SWE-Gym和SWE-bench Multilingual上，PAIChecker在四种LLM骨干网络上均取得最佳性能，二元准确率分别达到92.12%和91.67%。此外，研究发现SWE-bench Verified中13.6%的实例存在错位问题。

Conclusion: PAIChecker展示了准确、可泛化且渐进验证的错位检测能力，为未来SWE-bench类基准的可靠和可扩展构建提供了有效保障。

Abstract: SWE-bench-like benchmarks are widely used for evaluating LLM's issue resolution capability. They typically follow a common construction pipeline: each PR (Pull Request) is paired with its linked issue by extracting issue references from the PR description; the issue description is used as the problem statement, and the PR patch serves as the test oracle. However, due to the inherent complexity of developing and maintaining large repositories, such PR-Issue pairings are often misaligned in practice. In this work, we systematically study SWE-bench Verified instances, finding that 13.6% exhibit misalignment across five patterns in eleven fine-grained scenarios. To enable reliable and scalable construction of those benchmarks in the future, we propose PAIChecker, a multi-agent system for checking PR-Issue misalignment in SWE-bench-like benchmarks. Specifically, PAIChecker adopts a three-phase design that combines specific pattern identification, cross-agent label synthesis, and code-level validation, thereby enabling more accurate, generalizable, and progressively verified detection. Experiments on SWE-Gym and SWE-bench Multilingual show that PAIchecker achieves the best performance across all four LLM backbones, reaching up to 92.12% and 91.67% binary accuracy, respectively.

</details>


### [207] [Change2Task: From Repository Changes to Executable Coding Agent Tasks and Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28591)
*Haomin Qi,Xingliang Wang,Xuanqi Gao,Baihui Sang,Xin Zhang,Minghua Ma,Pengfei Gao,Yu Kang,Qingwei Lin,Saravan Rajmohan,Dongmei Zhang,Qi Zhang*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出Change2Task系统，利用代码仓库历史（合并的pull request）自动将开发者证据转化为可在现代健康版本上运行的已验证编码任务，覆盖五类常见任务族，验证成功率达79.6%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩展编码智能体的可执行数据供给面临瓶颈：每个任务需要耦合真实软件状态、规格说明、开发工具和可靠验证，而手工构建这类数据成本高昂且难以规模化。

Method: Change2Task基于仓库历史，将合并的PR转化为现代健康版本上的验证任务。核心包括：将历史证据与演化后的代码对齐；通过Patch Reversal、Code Mapping或Agent Reconstruction三种方式重建任务状态；验证从健康基态到任务态再到恢复态的完整生命周期。

Result: 在1,130个源变更上取得79.6%的验证任务构建成功率；相比基于PR的构建基线，在匹配候选集上多恢复29.2%的验证任务；智能体评估中历史与重建案例达成98.0%的匹配结果一致性；复用现代基态使全流程开销降低10.8%。

Conclusion: Change2Task通过从维持维护的真实环境中派生多个基于开发者证据的任务，提供了可用于编码智能体训练和评估的可执行数据，同时减少了重复环境搭建、存储和任务构建的开销。

Abstract: Scaling coding agents requires a continuing supply of executable data for training, benchmarking, and continuous evaluation. Each task must couple a realistic software state with a specification, development tools, and reliable verification. To expand this supply, we present Change2Task, a system grounded in repository history that converts merged pull requests into verified tasks on healthy modern revisions of the same repository. It aligns historical evidence with evolved code, reconstructs task states through Patch Reversal, Code Mapping, or Agent Reconstruction, and validates the lifecycle from a healthy base to a task state and a restored state. By deriving multiple tasks grounded in developer evidence from maintained environments, Change2Task provides executable data for coding agent training and evaluation while reducing repeated environment setup, storage, and task construction effort. We evaluate the system through five common and widely adopted coding agent task families: Bug Fix, Feature Addition, Test Generation, Application Programming Interface Migration, and Security Repair. Starting from 1,130 source changes eligible for construction, Change2Task achieves 79.6% verified task construction success across these task families. On a matched candidate set, it recovers 29.2% more verified tasks than a construction baseline based on pull requests. Historical and reconstructed cases achieve up to 98.0% matched outcome agreement under agent evaluation, while reuse of modern bases reduces measured expenditure across the complete pipeline by 10.8%.

</details>


<div id='cs.AI'></div>

# cs.AI [[Back]](#toc)

### [208] [Multi-Head Attention Residuals](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27230)
*Cheng Luo,Zefan Cai,Junjie Hu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出多头注意力残差（MHAR），将注意力残差中的路由查询重塑为H个逐子空间头，每个头拥有独立的深度历史softmax，以解决标准Transformer残差流中单一查询迫使所有特征子空间共享同一读取分布的问题。MHAR在100M、350M和1B规模上均优于标准Transformer及三种基线方法，验证损失分别提升-0.061、-0.149和-0.140，并支持从预训练模型无损转换进行8B中期训练。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准Transformer通过单一的加性残差流传播信息，注意力残差虽允许每个子层通过学习到的softmax读取深度历史，但所有特征子空间必须共享同一个查询分布。当不同子空间对读取哪些层存在分歧时（且分歧随模型宽度增长），这种强制折中会带来性能损失。

Method: 将注意力残差中的路由查询重塑为H个子空间头，每个头拥有独立的深度历史softmax，使得读取变为块对角结构。该重塑无需增加参数且计算开销可忽略，H=1时精确退化为原始注意力残差。同时开发了融合的Triton路由内核以提高训练吞吐量，并设计了一种恒等保持的转换方法（使用delta注意力残差）以支持从预训练模型进行中期训练。

Result: 在去重后的Nemotron语料上从头训练，MHAR在100M、350M和1B规模下验证损失分别提升-0.061、-0.149和-0.140，在所有设置中均为四种方法中的最优。通过直接探针实验确认了学习到的子空间分歧是性能提升的驱动因素。融合Triton内核将注意力残差训练吞吐量从基线0.2-0.5x提升到0.55-0.88x。8B中期训练在GSM8K上提升+3.2，GPQA上提升+3.1。

Conclusion: 头数量是一个真实的设计维度而非自由旋钮：验证损失对H呈U形，在H=4或8处存在平坦最优，过度拆分（H=16）会回吐部分增益。MHAR为大规模模型提供了有效的深度信息路由机制，在保持接近基线内存开销的同时显著提升性能。

Abstract: Transformers propagate information across depth through a single additive residual stream: every sublayer reads only the most recent state. Attention residuals relax this by letting each sublayer attend, through a learned softmax. However, that read uses a single query shared across the entire width, so every feature subspace must read the depth history through one distribution. The cost of this forced compromise grows with how much the subspaces disagree about which layers to read, and disagreement grows with model width. We introduce Multi-Head Attention Residuals (MHAR): the routing query is reshaped into H per-subspace heads, each with its own softmax over the depth history. The read becomes block-diagonal, the reshape adds zero parameters and negligible compute, and H = 1 recovers attention residuals exactly. Trained from scratch on a deduplicated Nemotron-based anneal corpus that is quality-filtered and STEM- and code-heavy, MHAR improves validation loss over a standard Transformer at 100M, 350M, and 1B (-0.061, -0.149, and -0.140). It achieves the best result among four methods in every setting, with the gain increasing from 100M to the larger scales. The head count is a real design axis rather than a free knob: validation loss is U-shaped with respect to H, with a flat optimum at H = 4 or H = 8 across scales. We adopt H = 8 for large-scale models; over-splitting beyond this point (H = 16) consistently gives back part of the gain. A direct probe of the trained queries confirms that learned subspace disagreement is the underlying driver. Fused Triton routing kernels increase attention-residual training throughput from 0.2-0.5x to 0.55-0.88x of the baseline while maintaining near-baseline peak memory. An identity-preserving conversion using delta attention residuals supports 8B mid-training, yielding improvements of +3.2 on GSM8K and +3.1 on GPQA.

</details>


### [209] [KernelGenBench: A Multi-Source and Multi-Chip Benchmark for LLM-based Kernel Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27231)
*Peiyu Zang,Jian Tao,Jialing Zhang,Yichen Yuan,Wentao Zhang,Guang Liu,Yonghua Lin*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出KernelGenBench，一个用于系统评估LLM和智能体生成的Triton内核的统一基准，涵盖多来源算子（210个）和多芯片平台（6个），并通过大规模评估（消耗超过150亿token）揭示了智能体方法优于纯LLM采样、跨平台性能严重退化以及自主内核生成成本高昂等关键发现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM显著提升了对高效加速器内核的需求，但内核开发仍高度专业化和劳动密集。尽管LLM和智能体框架在自动内核生成方面展现出潜力，但缺乏一个全面的基准来严格评估LLM生成的内核在不同算子来源和异构硬件平台上的表现。

Method: 构建KernelGenBench统一基准，包含两个互补子基准：KernelGenBench-MS（多来源），评估来自三个来源的210个算子（超越标准PyTorch中心任务）；KernelGenBench-MC（多芯片），使用110个算子子集衡量六个异构硬件平台上的性能可移植性。

Result: 大规模评估表明：(1) 基于智能体的方法始终优于纯LLM采样方法，cuBLAS算子对所有方法最具挑战性；(2) 生成性能在不同硬件平台上差异显著，即使是最近的内核专用智能体也经历严重的跨平台退化（如AutoKernel从NVIDIA的87%降到平台E的25%）；(3) 自主内核生成仍然成本高昂，专用智能体方法平均每个成功算子消耗511万token（AKO4all达519万），比简单LLM采样方法高出几个数量级。

Conclusion: KernelGenBench为LLM和智能体生成的内核提供了首个全面统一的评估框架，揭示了当前方法在跨平台性能、算子复杂度和生成成本方面的关键瓶颈，为未来内核自动生成研究指明了方向。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) have significantly increased the demand for efficient accelerator kernels, but kernel development remains a highly specialized and labor-intensive task. The recent rise of LLMs and agentic frameworks offers a promising pathway toward automatic kernel generation. However, despite rapid progress, there is still no comprehensive benchmark to rigorously evaluate LLM-generated kernels across diverse operator sources or heterogeneous hardware platforms. We present KernelGenBench, a unified benchmark for systematically evaluating LLM- and agent-generated Triton kernels across diverse operator sources and heterogeneous hardware platforms. It comprises two complementary sub-benchmarks: KernelGenBench-MS (Multi-Source), evaluating 210 operators from three sources beyond standard PyTorch-centric tasks, and KernelGenBench-MC (Multi-Chip), measuring performance portability across six heterogeneous hardware platforms using a 110-operator subset. Our large-scale evaluation, consuming over 15 billion tokens, shows: (1) agent-based methods consistently outperform pure LLM sampling methods, while cuBLAS operators are the most challenging across all methods; (2) generation performance varies significantly across hardware platforms, with even recent kernel-specialized agents experiencing severe cross-platform degradation (e.g., AutoKernel drops from 87% on NVIDIA to 25% on Platform E); (3) autonomous kernel generation remains highly cost-intensive, with specialized agent methods averaging 5.11 million tokens per successful operator (AKO4all reaches 5.19 million), orders of magnitude higher than simple LLM sampling approaches.

</details>


### [210] [RadHarmony: Radiological Data Handling in the Era of Agentic AI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27235)
*Frank Li,Bardia Khosravi,Mohammadreza Chavoshi,Theo Dapamede,YoungSeok Jeon,Janice Newsome,Hari Trivedi,Judy Gichoya*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: RadHarmony是一个开源的Python库，为放射影像数据集提供统一的API，支持加载、协调和数据增强，主要聚焦胸部X光片，并初步支持CT和MRI。它标准化了24个公开数据集，包装MONAI数据集以供深度学习使用，并引入AI-agent技能简化新数据集的集成流程。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在放射影像上训练深度学习模型需要整合来自不同来源、文件格式、目录结构、标签模式和标注类型的异构数据集，这增加了数据整合的复杂性和成本，需要一个统一的解决方案。

Method: RadHarmony通过四个关键手段实现统一：1) 将24个公开数据集的元数据标准化为单一表格格式；2) 包装MONAI的地图式数据集以供深度学习直接使用，支持可选的磁盘缓存；3) 通过单一接口支持分类标签、分割掩码、边界框和放射学报告文本；4) 提供交互式可视化工具以及AI-agent技能来引导新数据集的完整集成流程（从原始数据检查到代码生成和测试）。

Result: 通过预训练RadHarmony-ViT（一种参考视觉Transformer基线模型），结合了三个异构胸部X光数据集且无需数据集特定代码，验证了该库的实用性和有效性。

Conclusion: RadHarmony有效降低了放射影像数据集整合的门槛，为医学影像领域提供了一种标准化、可扩展的数据处理框架，代码和预训练模型权重已开源。

Abstract: Training deep learning models on radiological images requires integrating heterogeneous datasets across different sources, file formats, directory layouts, label schemas, and annotation types. We present RadHarmony, an open-source Python library that provides a unified API for loading, harmonizing, and augmenting radiological datasets, with a primary focus on chest radiographs and early support for computed tomography (CT) and magnetic resonance imaging (MRI). RadHarmony standardizes metadata from 24 public datasets into a single tabular format, wraps MONAI's map-style datasets for deep-learning-ready sample delivery with optional on-disk caching, and supports classification labels, segmentation masks, bounding boxes, and radiology report text through a single interface, with an interactive visualization tool for dataset exploration and verification. To lower the barrier for integrating new datasets, RadHarmony introduces an AI-agent skill that guides the full integration workflow from raw data inspection through code generation and testing. We demonstrate the library's utility by pretraining RadHarmony-ViT, a reference vision transformer baseline that combines three heterogeneous chest radiograph datasets with no dataset-specific code. The code and pretrained model weights are available at https://github.com/f10409/RadHarmony.

</details>


### [211] [Asymmetric Collapse in Model Merging: When Refusal Over- writes Recognition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27240)
*Aarnav Choudhary,Matheus Fonseca Rocha,Jiwon Seo,Vasu Sharma,Maheep Chaudhary*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究了四种标准模型合并方法（Linear、SLERP、TIES、DARE-TIES）在合并两个互补安全目标微调模型（CARES危害分级和WildJailbreak对抗拒答）时的表现。结果显示，攻击抵抗能力的迁移（81-85%拒答率）远优于分类准确率（最高仅12.9%）。这种不对称性并非源于任务向量的强对抗（余弦相似度仅0.011），而是由于拒答微调产生更大的每层任务向量幅度，导致对幅度敏感的合并方法偏向拒答更新。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 模型合并常用于无需额外训练即可组合多个微调模型的能力，但标准合并方法能否同时保留多个安全相关行为尚不明确。研究旨在通过受控案例研究回答此问题。

Method: 使用两个Gemma-3-1B-IT微调模型（CARES危害分级与WildJailbreak对抗拒答），分别用Linear、SLERP、TIES和DARE-TIES四种方法合并，并评估分类准确率、攻击抵抗和良性合规三个维度。

Result: 四种合并方法均显示攻击抵抗迁移显著优于分类准确率：拒答率保留81-85%，而CARES准确率最多仅12.9%。任务向量接近正交（余弦相似度0.011），说明不对称性并非由方向冲突导致，而是拒答微调产生更大的任务向量幅度所致。

Conclusion: 标准模型合并可能将安全识别退化为宽泛拒答，尤其当安全相关任务向量在规模上差异显著时。这提示合并方法需考虑任务向量的幅度差异，而非仅关注方向关系。

Abstract: Model merging is often used to combine capabilities from separately fine-tuned models without additional training, but it is unclear whether standard merging methods preserve multiple safety-relevant behaviors simultaneously. We study this question through a controlled case study using two Gemma-3-1B-IT finetunes on two complementary safety objectives: CARES harm-level classification and WildJailbreak adversarial refusal. We merge the two fine-tunes using Linear, SLERP, TIES, and DARE-TIES, and evaluate the merged models on classification accuracy, attack resistance, and benign compliance. Across all four methods, attack resistance transfers significantly more than classification accuracy: merged models retain 81-85% jailbreak refusal rates while CARES accuracy falls to at most 12.9%. Weight-space measurements suggest that this asymmetry is not caused by strongly opposing task-vector directions: the two task vectors are nearly orthogonal (cosine similarity 0.011). Instead, the refusal fine-tune induces consistently larger per-layer task-vector magnitudes, causing magnitude-sensitive methods to favor refusal updates. These results show that standard model merging can collapse safety recognition into broad refusal when safety-relevant task vectors differ substantially in scale.

</details>


### [212] [Divergence Decoding: Training-Free Capability Fusion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27248)
*Yimi Wang,Hao Li,Shuo Yang,He Cao,Dechen Zhang,Ziang Wu,Zhiyuan Yan,Fanyang Mo,Li Yuan*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出Divergence Decoding，一种无需训练的推理时框架，通过监控专用模型与通用模型在每个token上的Jensen-Shannon散度，动态路由到更合适的模型，实现通用推理能力与领域专业知识的融合。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型语言模型（通用模型）擅长推理但缺乏专业领域知识，而领域专用模型（专用模型）具备专业知识却存在逻辑能力下降和鲁棒性减弱的副作用。如何同时获得两种模型的优势是一个亟待解决的难题。

Method: 借鉴投机解码（speculative decoding）的"草稿-验证"框架，将其重构为自适应路由机制。核心是利用Jensen-Shannon散度监控两个模型在每个token上的分布分歧，当专用模型表现出显著分歧时，判断为潜在推理风险并立即将控制权路由给通用模型，从而实现动态的通用推理注入与领域专业知识保留。

Result: 在GPQA、ChemBench和ChemCoTBench等科学基准上，对Qwen和Llama系列模型进行评估，Divergence Decoding的表现优于领域专用模型和通用模型，超越了大多数单模型基线。

Conclusion: Divergence Decoding提供了一种通用的、无需训练的范式，通过自适应推理时协作来实现不同LLM能力的融合，为解决通用模型与专用模型的能力取舍问题提供了新思路。

Abstract: While large language models excel in reasoning, these generalists often lack knowledge for specialized scientific domains. Conversely, domain models~(specialists), while knowledgeable, suffer from specialization side-effects including diminished logic and reduced robustness.To address this dilemma, we introduce Divergence Decoding, a training-free framework for capability fusion. It reconstructs the "draft-and-verify" skeleton of speculative decoding into an adaptive routing mechanism. The core is using Jensen-Shannon divergence to monitor the distributional disagreement between the two models at each token. When the specialist exhibits significant divergence, our method identifies it as a potential reasoning risk and instantaneously routes control to the generalist. This allows the dynamic injection of general reasoning while preserving domain expertise, achieving inference-time policy composition of the generalist and the specialist.We evaluate Divergence Decoding across diverse model families (Qwen and Llama series) on challenging scientific benchmarks (GPQA, ChemBench, and ChemCoTBench). Experimental results demonstrate that Divergence Decoding outperforms both the domain-specialized and general-purpose models, effectively surpassing the performance of most single-model baseline. This suggests that Divergence Decoding provides a general, training-free paradigm for fusing diverse LLM capabilities through adaptive inference-time collaboration.

</details>


### [213] [PIE-APT: A Unified Framework for Temporal Planning and Contradiction Hunting via Incremental Direct-Derivation Abduction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27287)
*Amir Hossein Sharafi,Alireza Shahbazi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出一个统一框架，包含PIE-Abducer（增量直接推导溯因）和PIE-APT（时序知识图谱的溯因规划）两个模块，在描述逻辑（DL）上原生操作，解决动态知识图谱中开放世界环境下的推理与规划问题。该方法通过黑盒增量推理器和非单调更新保持逻辑可判定性，并通过直接推导溯因绕过传统最小击中集（MHS）枚举的组合搜索。实验在四个OWL基准上证明了相对经典规划器的定性优势和相对MHS基线的定量优势。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 动态知识图谱上的推理与规划在开放世界（信息不完整）环境中面临重大挑战：现有动作形式化常遇到可判定性问题与Ramification Problem；通过结构溯因管理不完整知识需要大规模的组合搜索。

Method: 框架包含两个集成模块：PIE-Abducer利用在一致分支中注入目标逻辑否定并提取反演后果来获取缺失前提，避免MHS枚举；PIE-APT采用递归的Generate-and-Test架构，将反向链A*搜索与PIE-Abducer交错进行（直至有界因果深度），再通过正向链时序投影严格验证。状态转换沿线性时间轴建模为对演绎封闭DL理论的非单调更新。

Result: 在四个强调经典规划不具备的语义能力的OWL基准上（参数化目标与见证搜索、搜索中DL蕴含、开放世界假设注入、对抗性矛盾寻找），实验显示该方法在定性上优于经典规划器，且直接推导溯因方法在溯因丰富阶段定量优于MHS忠实的基线方法。

Conclusion: 将增量推理器视为黑盒并在OWL中原生表示动作（无需外部模态算子）保持了逻辑可判定性；直接推导溯因比传统组合语法搜索更高效，为动态知识图谱上的开放世界规划提供了可行且可判定的解决方案。

Abstract: Reasoning and planning over Dynamic Knowledge Graphs (DKGs) present significant challenges, especially in open-world environments with incomplete information. Existing action formalisms often face decidability issues and the Ramification Problem, while managing incomplete knowledge via structural abduction requires expansive combinatorial search. This paper introduces a unified framework with two integrated modules---\textbf{PIE-Abducer} (incremental direct-derivation abduction) and \textbf{PIE-APT} (Abductive Planning for Temporal KGs)---operating natively on the highly expressive Description Logic. We model state transitions along a linear timeline as non-monotonic updates to deductively closed DL theories. Treating the incremental reasoner as a black-box and representing actions natively in OWL without external modal operators preserves logical decidability. To address incomplete knowledge, \textbf{PIE-Abducer} circumvents traditional Minimal Hitting Set (MHS) enumeration. Instead of combinatorial syntactic search, it injects the logical negation of a target goal into a consistent branch and extracts missing premises via direct refutation consequences. \textbf{PIE-APT} then employs a recursive \textit{Generate-and-Test} architecture, interleaving backward-chaining A* search with \textbf{PIE-Abducer} up to a bounded causal depth, followed by strict validation via forward-chaining Temporal Projection. We evaluate four OWL benchmarks stressing semantic abilities absent in classical planning: parameterized goals with witness search, mid-search DL entailment, open-world assumption injection, and adversarial contradiction hunting. Results demonstrate qualitative superiority over classical planners and prove our direct-derivation approach quantitatively outperforms an MHS-faithful baseline during abductive enrichment.

</details>


### [214] [HALO: Heterogeneous Admission through Localized Obligations for Safe Agentic Execution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27636)
*Taewoo Park,Kyeonghyun Yoo,Kiseok Kim,Seunghyun Yoo,Hwangnam Kim*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出HALO（带局部义务的异构准入）运行时协议，解决智能体系统在外部使用前条件变化导致响应组件不再兼容的问题。HALO保留前提仍受支持的组件，每次派发前重新检查精确动作，并允许被阻止的动作仅由新候选替换。实验表明HALO匹配全部96个准入期望、通过全部20个协议测试，在结构响应回放中保留248/248受支持组件（整响应策略为0/248），并在PX4/Gazebo冷启动会话中阻止了所有过期路线并完成全部恢复。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的智能体AI系统可能返回包含通知、请求、交接和动作的异构响应，但外部使用前条件可能变化，同响应的组件之间不一定保持兼容。整响应拒绝会丢弃有用组件，而独立检查可能导致依赖组件缺少其前提，因此需要一种运行时协议来精细地管理组件准入。

Method: HALO（Heterogeneous Admission with Localized Obligations）运行时协议，其核心策略包括：(1) 保留前提仍受支持的组件；(2) 每次派发前重新检查每个精确动作；(3) 允许被阻止的动作仅由新鲜候选（fresh candidates）替换。

Result: HALO匹配了全部96个准入期望，通过了全部20个协议测试。在结构响应回放中保留了248/248受支持组件（包括128/128不受无关变化影响的组件），而整响应策略仅保留0/248。在十个PX4/Gazebo冷启动会话中，HALO阻止了所有测试的过期路线，未观察到匹配的过期设定点，并完成了所有新鲜恢复。

Conclusion: HALO提供了一种优于整响应策略的精细准入机制，能在条件变化时保留有用组件同时阻止失效组件，并通过重新检查和新鲜候选替换机制保障协议安全性，在无人机冷启动等实际场景中验证了有效性。

Abstract: Recent agentic AI systems may return a heterogeneous response containing notices, requests, handoffs, and actions. Conditions can change before external use, so components from the same response need not remain supported together. Rejecting the whole response discards useful components, whereas checking components independently can leave a dependent without its prerequisite. We present Heterogeneous Admission with Localized Obligations (HALO), a runtime protocol that preserves supported components whose declared prerequisites also remain supported, rechecks each exact action before dispatch, and allows blocked actions to be replaced only by fresh candidates. HALO matched all 96 admission expectations and passed all 20 protocol tests. In structured-response replay, it retained 248/248 supported components, including 128/128 unaffected by unrelated changes, while a whole-response policy retained 0/248. Across ten cold-start PX4/Gazebo sessions, HALO blocked every tested stale route, observed no matching stale setpoint, and completed all fresh recoveries.

</details>


### [215] [PAUSE: A User-Centric Benchmark for Personal AI Assistants in Unified Service Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27354)
*Haoyu Chen,Xirui Shi,Yuyao Wang,Jerry Chen,Di Niu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: PAUSE是一个以用户为中心的个人AI助手基准测试，在状态化、服务集成的环境中评估AI助手，涵盖跨服务的动作协调、状态一致性维护和授权约束处理。实验表明即使最先进的专有模型在需要状态推理和配置感知的任务上完成率也不足70%，并揭示了可解释的失败模式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基准测试往往将服务上下文碎片化或抽象掉用户状态，无法在真实的用户中心服务设置中评估个人AI助手行为。实际部署中的助手需要推理持久用户状态、尊重用户特定配置和权限、并在多个服务间维持长时程约束感知交互，而现有评估体系缺乏对此类能力的全面检验。

Method: PAUSE基准包含三个核心部分：(1) 状态化、服务集成的评估环境，要求智能体跨异构用户自有资源协调操作，同时保持与环境状态和授权约束的一致性；(2) 通过真实用户模拟实现显式用户-智能体交互，超越静态工具执行的评估；(3) 多机制评估框架——开放型服务管理任务采用语义和轨迹级行为指标，约束密集型任务使用确定性、基于状态的验证。此外还提出了用户中心的合成流水线，用于规模化生成连贯的服务环境、用户配置和可靠标注的任务。

Result: 基准测试结果显示，即使最先进的专有模型在需要状态推理和配置感知的场景中任务完成率也未达到70%。分析揭示了模型存在一致且可解释的失败模式，表明当前主流模型在个人AI助手的关键能力上仍存在显著差距。

Conclusion: PAUSE为评估个人AI助手在真实服务场景中的表现提供了更全面的基准，通过合成流水线支持基准的可扩展性和未来研究方向的探索，并为改进AI助手的状态推理和配置感知能力指明了方向。

Abstract: Personal AI assistants are increasingly deployed as task-oriented, tool-augmented agents that operate within unified service environments to support everyday user activities. In realistic settings, such assistants must reason over persistent user state, respect user-specific configurations and permissions, and sustain long-horizon, constraint-aware interactions across multiple services. Existing benchmarks, however, often fragment service contexts or abstract away user state, limiting their ability to evaluate user-centric personal assistant behavior in realistic service settings. We introduce PAUSE, a user-centric benchmark for evaluating personal AI assistants in stateful, service-integrated environments. PAUSE captures core challenges of real-world assistant deployment by requiring agents to coordinate actions across heterogeneous user-owned resources while maintaining consistency with environment state, authorization constraints over multi-turn interactions. The benchmark incorporates explicit user-agent interaction via realistic user simulation, enabling evaluation beyond static tool execution. To support principled and reproducible evaluation, PAUSE adopts a multi-regime evaluation framework aligned with task characteristics. Open-ended service management tasks are assessed using semantic and trajectory-level behavioral metrics, while constraint-intensive tasks admit deterministic, state-based verification. Benchmark results show that even state-of-the-art proprietary models fail to reach 70% task completion on scenarios requiring stateful reasoning and configuration awareness, revealing consistent and interpretable failure patterns. Finally, we present a user-centric synthesis pipeline that enables scalable generation of coherent service environments, user configurations, and reliably annotated tasks, supporting benchmark extensibility and future research.

</details>


### [216] [Old Tricks, New Models: How Simple Image Transformations Break Modern AI-based Content Moderation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28187)
*Marco Alecci,Francesco Marchiori,Iyiola Emmanuel Olatunji,Tegawendé F. Bissyandé,Jacques Klein*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文对三个商业多模态图片审核API进行了大规模黑盒鲁棒性评估，发现简单的图像变换（如颜色反转、灰度化）无需梯度或替代模型即可绕过这些系统，且不同数据集和危害类别下鲁棒性差异显著，结论是仅用基础模型API替换传统审核分类器并不能提供可靠的安全边界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统审核分类器政策覆盖有限且上下文理解不足，而基于大型基础模型的商业多模态审核API被宣称具有更广的覆盖范围和更强的能力。本文旨在验证这种转变是否真的带来了更鲁棒的图像审核性能。

Method: 对三个成熟的商业图像审核服务进行大规模黑盒评估，使用七种简单且与模型无关的图像变换，跨多个提供商、数据集、危害类别、感知相似性约束和变换强度进行系统性测试。

Result: （1）三个商业服务均可通过无需梯度、替代模型或目标系统知识的廉价图像变换被绕过；（2）即使是固定的变换（如颜色反转和灰度化）也能诱导不安全到安全的决策转变，同时内容对人类仍可辨识；（3）不同数据集和危害类别间的鲁棒性差异显著，多模态内容和自伤类别存在明显脆弱性。

Conclusion: 用基础模型API替代传统审核分类器本身并不能提供可靠的安全边界。此类系统必须在实际变换下进行评估，并应作为分层审核流水线的一个组件部署，而非作为独立的安全过滤器。

Abstract: While automated content-moderation systems have become essential for screening harmful content at scale, conventional task-specific classifiers often provide limited policy cov- erage and contextual understanding. Recently, commercial multimodal moderation APIs built on large foundation models have been introduced with the promise of providing broader and more capable safety filters. In this work, we analyze whether this shift also yields more robust image moderation. We conduct a large-scale black-box evaluation on three established commercial image-moderation services and compare their robustness. By evaluating seven simple, model-agnostic image transformations across multiple providers, datasets, harm categories, perceptual-similarity constraints, and transformation intensities, we find that: (1) all three commercial services can be bypassed using inexpensive image transformations that require no gradients, surrogate models, or knowledge of the target system; (2) even fixed transformations such as color inversion and grayscale conversion induce unsafe-to-safe decision changes while preserving content that remains recognizable to humans; (3) their robustness varies substantially across datasets and harm categories, with multimodal content and self-harm exhibiting pronounced vulnerabilities. This yields the conclusion that replacing conventional moderation classifiers with foundation-model-based APIs does not, by itself, provide a reliable security boundary. Such systems must be evaluated under realistic transformations and deployed as one component of a layered moderation pipeline rather than as standalone safety filters.

</details>


### [217] [SkillMentor: LLM Agent Self-Evolution via Learning Blind-Spot Diagnosis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27360)
*Xiaoyi Bao,Yuanzhen Xie,Yunzhi Tan,Jinghang Gu,Zhongqing Wang,Chu-Ren Huang,Bo Hu,Zang Li*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出SkillMentor框架，将"盲点诊断"（发现agent不知道什么）视为独立于执行的可学习能力。通过强化学习训练一个Mentor策略，用于生成诊断任务、识别重复失败模式并将其整理为可复用的纠正技能。在AppWorld和BFCLv3基准上，将执行器性能平均提升44.2%，且不依赖执行器权重更新或人工标注数据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有Agent自我进化研究主要聚焦于"学会行动"，而忽视了同样重要的能力——"学会发现自身不知道什么"。已有方法通常假设失败发现是给定的，只关注如何在识别失败后进行修复。作者质疑：盲点诊断本身是否可以学习？这需要将诊断与执行分离，排除执行器适应和人工监督两个替代性进步来源，从而严格检验诊断能力的可学习性。

Method: 提出SkillMentor框架，通过强化学习训练一个Mentor策略。该策略有三个核心功能：(1) 生成诊断任务以主动探测执行器的盲点；(2) 识别重复出现的失败模式；(3) 将这些失败模式整理成可复用的纠正技能。整个过程中，执行器的权重保持不变，也不依赖人类标注示例，所有性能提升均源自学习到的诊断能力。

Result: 在AppWorld和BFCLv3两个基准测试上，SkillMentor将执行器性能平均提升44.2%。这一结果表明，盲点诊断确实是一种可学习的独立能力。

Conclusion: 盲点诊断是可学习的，Agent可以在不更新执行器权重、不依赖人工策划数据的情况下实现自我进化。这为Agent自我进化开辟了新的研究方向——从"学会行动"拓展到"学会认知自身局限"。

Abstract: Agent self-evolution has primarily focused on learning how to act, while overlooking an equally important capability: learning to discover what an agent does not know. Existing approaches typically assume that failure discovery is given, focusing on how to repair failures once they are identified. We ask whether blind-spot diagnosis itself can be learned. We thus study diagnosis as an agent capability separate from execution, and exclude two alternative sources of progress: executor adaptation and human supervision. Under these constraints, performance cannot improve through executor updates or annotated examples, forcing all improvements to originate from the learned diagnostic capability. We propose SkillMentor, which trains a Mentor policy via reinforcement learning to generate diagnostic tasks, identify recurrent failure modes, and curate them into reusable corrective skills. Across AppWorld and BFCLv3, SkillMentor improves executor performance by an average of 44.2%. These results suggest that blind-spot diagnosis is a learnable capability, enabling self-evolution without updating executor weights or relying on human-curated data.

</details>


### [218] [Beyond the Bidirectional Promise: Re-evaluating the Robustness of Diffusion Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27386)
*Saurabh Yadav,Badri Narayana Patro,Vijay Srinivas Agneeswaran*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文系统评估了扩散语言模型（DLM）在自然噪声和对抗攻击下的鲁棒性与校准性能，通过与自回归模型进行参数匹配对比，发现DLM虽然天然抵抗梯度对抗攻击，但在自然噪声下并不更鲁棒，且存在系统性过度自信问题；机制探针揭示所有模型的脆弱性源于解码器路由失败而非输入编码问题，最终结论是DLM鲁棒性必须内建到迭代解码循环中而非表面修补。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩散语言模型作为自回归生成的有力替代方案，支持双向上下文和迭代精炼，但其在自然输入噪声和对抗攻击下的可靠性尚未被充分研究。理解DLM的鲁棒性和校准特性对实际部署至关重要。

Method: 使用两对参数匹配的模型组合（LLaDA-8B vs. LLaMA-3-8B 和 Dream-7B vs. Qwen2.5-7B），在32种自然扰动条件下进行评估，并结合对抗梯度探针和机制性隐藏状态分析。通过配对设计隔离架构固有属性与权重依赖行为。

Result: DLM呈现细微的鲁棒性特征：高随机性的损失景观天然抵抗基于梯度的对抗后缀，但对自然噪声无保证防御；日常鲁棒性依赖权重而非架构。DLM表现出系统性过度自信，构成实际部署隐患。机制探针显示所有模型完美编码输入损坏，行为脆弱性完全源于解码器路由失败。表面提示修补无法改善噪声基线性能。

Conclusion: DLM的鲁棒性无法通过表面修补获得，必须从根本上集成到迭代解码循环之中。

Abstract: Diffusion Language Models (DLMs) offer a compelling alternative to autoregressive (AR) generation by enabling bidirectional context and iterative refinement. However, their reliability under natural input noise and adversarial attacks remains under-explored. To address this, we systematically evaluate DLM robustness and calibration against AR baselines, using two parameter-matched pairs (LLaDA-8B vs. LLaMA-3-8B and Dream-7B vs. Qwen2.5-7B) across 32 natural perturbation conditions, adversarial gradient probes, and mechanistic hidden-state analyses. This paired design effectively isolates architecture-intrinsic properties from weight-dependent behaviors. We find a nuanced robustness profile: while highly stochastic DLM loss landscapes naturally resist gradient-based adversarial suffixes, they provide no guaranteed defense against natural noise, proving that everyday robustness is weight-dependent rather than inherently architectural. Furthermore, DLMs exhibit systematic overconfidence, presenting a practical deployment hazard. Most crucially, mechanistic probing reveals that all models perfectly encode input corruption, isolating behavioral fragility entirely to a decoder routing failure. Consistent with this diagnosis, we show that surface-level prompt patching fails to improve over noisy baselines. Ultimately, DLM robustness cannot be patched on; it must be fundamentally integrated into the iterative decoding loop.

</details>


### [219] [Bridging Inference-Time Scaling and Episodic Memory with Action-Centric Graphs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27415)
*Xu Zheng,Chaohao Lin,Zhuomin Chen,Weijieying Ren,Haifeng Chen,Wei Cheng,Dongsheng Luo*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出GAMER框架，通过构建动态"以行动为中心的图"（Action-Centric Graph）来建模历史推理过程，将记忆机制从LLM中解耦，并通过双流时序差分学习估计行动节点的正负价值，从而在推理阶段双向优化决策，显著提升推理扩展的效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 推理时扩展虽增强了LLM的复杂推理能力，但现有方法以无状态方式运行，存在冗余搜索的低效问题。已有的记忆机制过度依赖LLM的推理能力，导致计算成本过高。

Method: 提出GAMER框架：1) 将历史推理建模为动态的行动中心图；2) 将记忆机制与LLM解耦以减少token/金钱消耗；3) 使用双流时序差分（TD）学习机制分别估计行动节点的正向(建议)和负向(规避)价值；4) 推理阶段通过学习到的价值函数双向优化决策（正向提供行动建议，负向标示高风险行动）并在图上高效搜索。

Result: 在多种基准测试上，GAMER相比原始基线在成功率/进度率上分别提升20.81%/6.17%。

Conclusion: GAMER通过将推断扩展与情景记忆有效结合，在降低计算成本的同时显著提升智能体的推理效率和性能，实现了记忆机制与LLM的解耦。

Abstract: Recent advancements in inference-time scaling have significantly unlocked the complex reasoning capabilities of Large Language Models~(LLMs). However, for agents, these approaches suffer from a critical inefficiency, operating in a stateless manner and engaging in redundant search processes. Existing memory mechanisms largely rely on the reasoning capabilities of LLMs, leading to prohibitive computational costs. In this paper, we propose a novel framework, \textit{GAMER}~(Graph-based Action-centric Memory with Episodic Reasoning), that bridges the gap between inference scaling and episodic memory. Our approach models historical reasoning as a dynamic \textit{Action-Centric Graph}. By decoupling the memory mechanism from LLMs, our method can save token/money usage by providing less memory context than memory mechanism baselines. To extract knowledge from the graph effectively, we use a dual-stream Temporal Difference learning mechanism to estimate the positive~(suggestion) and negative~(avoidance) value of action nodes based on past successes and failures. During the inference phase, this learned value function optimizes decision-making bi-directionally, so that positive values provide action suggestions, while negative values indicate high-risk actions. By performing efficient searches on the graph, our method significantly improves the efficiency of inference scaling. Experiments on multiple benchmarks demonstrate that \textit{GAMER} achieves superior performance by \textbf{20.81\%/6.17\%} for success/progress rate compared to vanilla baselines.

</details>


### [220] [Dimensionality and Measurement Precision in HLE's Multiple-Choice Subset](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27420)
*Mayank Sharma,Savira Nadela,Tyler Matteson*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文使用项目反应理论（IRT）对Humanity's Last Exam（HLE）基准测试进行心理测量学分析，发现HLE仅测量单一的一般推理因子，其领域子分数不具有区分性意义，且测试信息函数显示其对最强大模型的区分能力有限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: HLE作为评估前沿大语言模型的重要基准，其八个学科领域子分数常被解读为不同能力的证据，但从未有研究验证这些标签是否对应经验上可分离的潜在构念，也未评估该基准能否有效区分能力相近的模型。

Method: 对29个LLM在HLE文本多选子集（428题）上的表现，拟合双参数逻辑斯蒂克IRT模型，综合运用McDonald's ω_h、域标签解释方差比例、残差相关性分析、域特定能力估计与总分相关性等方法评估维度性，并通过测试信息函数分析测量精度分布。

Result: 发现HLE测量单一一般推理因子（ω_h=0.998），域标签仅解释3.5%的项目反应方差，域内和域间残差相关性几乎相同（Cohen's d=0.016），域特定能力估计与总分高度冗余（r≥0.81）。测量精度集中在中等能力水平，在θ=0以上急剧下降，恰好落在前沿模型所在区间。

Conclusion: HLE的领域子分数不构成独立能力证据，该基准对最强模型之间的区分能力有限，建议研究者谨慎解读HLE的领域维度结果。

Abstract: Humanity's Last Exam (HLE) is widely used to evaluate frontier language models. HLE organizes its questions into eight subject-domain categories, whose subscores are often interpreted as evidence of distinct capabilities. However, no study has assessed whether these labels correspond to empirically separable latent constructs, nor whether the benchmark effectively differentiates between models of similar ability. We evaluate 29 LLMs on the text-only multiple-choice subset of HLE ($J = 428$ items) and apply psychometric methods to assess both the dimensionality of the benchmark and the distribution of its measurement precision. Fitting a two-parameter logistic IRT model, we find convergent evidence that HLE measures a single general reasoning factor: McDonald's $ω_h = 0.998$, domain labels explain only 3.5\% of item response variance, within- and between-domain residual correlations are nearly identical (Cohen's $d = 0.016$), and domain-specific ability estimates are near-redundant with the total score ($r \geq 0.81$). A separate analysis of the test information function reveals that measurement precision concentrates at moderate ability levels and drops sharply above $θ= 0$, where frontier models sit. These findings suggest that HLE's domain subscores do not warrant distinct capability interpretations and that the benchmark's ability to discriminate among the strongest models is limited.

</details>


### [221] [Leveraging Trajectory Graphs for Pre-Execution Error Diagnosis in Agentic LLM Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27443)
*Xu Zheng,Zhuomin Chen,Chaohao Lin,Hua Wei,Haifeng Chen,Wei Cheng,Dongsheng Luo*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出Trajectory Graph Copilot框架，通过将历史轨迹建模为概率图并用图神经网络诊断潜在动作错误，在动作执行前为LLM智能体提供早期警告，从而帮助其完成长期交互任务。在四个基准测试上平均提升14.69%的通过率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM智能体在处理长期交互任务时常常因巨大的动作空间和累积误差而失败——一个次优动作就可能使整个轨迹偏离，耗尽有限的步数预算在低效或不可恢复的路径上。现有的微调方法成本高昂，需要一种无需昂贵微调的解决方案。

Method: 受软件调试中分析执行日志以预先捕获错误的启发，该方法包含两个核心组件：(1) Graph Debugger：将历史轨迹建模为概率图，用图神经网络识别频繁导致失败的序列化动作模式；(2) 作为主动诊断沙箱，在执行前对可能有缺陷的动作发出早期警告，提示智能体自我纠正，实现动作前错误诊断。

Result: 在四个基准测试上使用三种LLM智能体进行广泛实验，平均提升了14.69%的通过率。

Conclusion: 通过动作前错误诊断来防止代价高昂的失误，显著增强了LLM智能体成功完成长期交互任务的能力，为无需微调即可提升智能体性能提供了一种新的有效途径。

Abstract: Large Language Model~(LLM)-based agents have demonstrated exceptional performance across a wide range of complex interactive tasks. However, they often struggle with long-horizon interactive tasks common in domains, such as embodied AI. The complexity and vast action spaces in these settings lead to compounding errors, where a single suboptimal action can derail an entire trajectory, causing the agent to exhaust its limited step budget on inefficient or unrecoverable paths. To overcome this without costly fine-tuning, we draw inspiration from software debugging, where execution logs are analyzed to preemptively catch errors. We propose \textit{Trajectory Graph Copilot}, a novel framework that acts as a ``copilot'' for LLM agents by diagnosing potential action errors before they are executed. At its core,\textit{Graph Debugger} models historical trajectories as a probabilistic graph and uses a Graph Neural Network to identify sequential action patterns that frequently lead to failure. Functioning as a proactive diagnostic sandbox, our method provides early warnings on potentially flawed actions, prompting the agent to self-correct. This pre-action error diagnosis prevents costly mistakes, significantly enhancing the agent's ability to complete long-horizon tasks successfully. The extensive experiments on four benchmarks with three LLM agents demonstrate a $14.69\%$ pass ratio improvement on average.

</details>


### [222] [VAmoS Bench: Voice Agent Simulation Bench](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27453)
*Joshua Meyer,Sahar Shayegan,Ritiz Tambi,Ali Khan,Sun Kim,Victor Shih,Mehdi Jamei,Andi Partovi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: VAmoS Bench 是一个用于端到端评估完整语音代理系统（如客服电话）的基准测试平台。它通过100个模拟场景、真实SQL后端和二进制断言，衡量语音代理在客户支持任务中是否正确处理了通话（含拒接或转接等正确结果），填补了现有基准只关注组件质量而忽略整体任务完成度的空白。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有语音代理基准大多衡量组件质量和对话属性（如词错误率、延迟、自然度、话轮转换），但很少评估代理是否能自主正确地处理一通电话。呼叫中心将此称为"包容率"（containment），即自动化系统无需转接人工即可解决的电话比例。部分电话的正确结果可能是拒绝或转接，现有基准无法覆盖这一需求缺口。

Method: 构建一个状态化的客户支持任务场景：代理角色为Riley（虚构银行的信用卡客服），可冻结、取消、更换或激活卡片。平台包含100个场景，每个场景提供具有私有目标的模拟呼叫者和预置的PostgreSQL后端。代理可使用五个工具执行真实SQL操作。每个场景定义了二进制断言，由评分器根据完整交互轨迹（包括对话内容、工具调用、参数及返回行）进行评估。约三分之一的场景施加对抗性压力。

Result: 该基准能够捕获两类关键失败：代理声称修改了卡片但未更新数据库，以及代理做出了正确的数据库更改但泄露了受保护信息。评估协议支持不断演进的排行榜，可在同一版本上评估更多语音代理，后续版本可扩展任务和场景。

Conclusion: VAmoS Bench 是首个聚焦金融服务领域的端到端语音代理系统基准，通过真实工具调用、数据库状态验证和二进制断言，为衡量语音代理的完整任务处理能力（含正确拒绝和转接）提供了标准化评估框架，并为未来的任务扩展保留了演进空间。

Abstract: Production voice agents span cascaded, speech-to-speech, and hybrid architectures. Voice-agent benchmarks typically measure component quality and conversational properties such as word error rate, latency, naturalness, and turn-taking. Fewer measure whether the agent handled a phone call correctly on its own. Contact centers refer to this as ``containment'': the share of phone calls the automated system resolves without handing off to a human. On some phone calls the right outcome is refusal or a redirect. To address this gap, we introduce VAmoS Bench, the Voice Agent Simulation Bench. It measures complete voice-agent systems end to end in a stateful customer-support task. The agent is Riley, a credit-card support representative for a fictional bank who can freeze, cancel, replace, or activate a card. Each of 100 scenarios supplies a simulated caller with a private goal and a seeded PostgreSQL backend. The platform uses each scenario to populate and activate an isolated simulation in which the caller reaches Riley over audio; roughly one-third apply adversarial pressure. The agent can use five tools that execute real SQL against the backend. Each scenario also defines binary assertions. A grader evaluates them against the complete trace of what the caller and agent said and what the agent did, including tool invocations, arguments, and returned rows. This catches an agent that claims to have changed a card without updating the database, as well as one that makes the right database change while disclosing protected information. This first benchmark version focuses on financial services. Its evaluation protocol supports an evolving leaderboard: additional voice agents can be evaluated on the same version, while later versions can expand the tasks and scenarios.

</details>


### [223] [Skill Use or Skill Theater? Evaluating the Reasoning Backroom in Skill-Augmented Language Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27484)
*Jinwei Hu,Yi Qi,Xinmiao Huang,Youcheng Sun,Yi Dong,Xiaowei Huang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出BACKTRACE评估框架，通过匹配反事实干预来揭示技能增强型语言代理中"推理后台"现象——即代理声称使用技能与实际因果依赖之间存在系统性差距。研究发现跨模型和领域普遍存在来源失败，观测性检测器无法识别真实依赖，多代理系统中技能影响可能脱离来源传播。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有评估通常从可见推理或代理自身归因推断技能使用，但这些信号只显示代理"看起来"使用了什么，无法证明技能是否真正改变了其决策。需要一种干预式的评估方法来检验技能的实际因果影响。

Method: 提出BACKTRACE框架：将每个技能条件下的回答与匹配的无技能反事实配对，对技能的意义、措辞、身份、内容和分配进行干预，并在答案确定后才获取归因。在此基础上构建BACKROOMBench测试平台，涵盖受控逻辑和竞赛数学、多种技能条件、单代理和多代理设置及多种模型家族。

Result: 跨模型和领域发现普遍的来源失败：声称的技能使用稳定而因果依赖和符号效用变化，产生静默采纳和表演性使用。行为效应更可靠地跟随程序内容而非显示的技能身份，而归因对人工制品的可用性反应强烈。观测性检测器（直接声明、文本提及、轨迹相似性、LLM裁判）均无法识别真实依赖。多代理系统中技能影响可在来源丢失后继续传播，而无技能团队仍会命名从未提供的技能和来源。

Conclusion: "推理后台"是一个普遍的AI来源/provenance问题，其审计必须依赖干预手段而非观测信号。

Abstract: Reusable skills are becoming a standard interface for extending language agents with task procedures. Yet evaluators usually infer skill use from visible reasoning or the agent's own attribution. These signals show what the agent appears to use, not whether the skill changed its decision. We ask whether skill-augmented agents exhibit a \textbf{Reasoning Backroom}, a systematic gap between stated skill use and intervention-measured influence. We introduce BACKTRACE, an evaluation framework that pairs each skill-conditioned answer with a matched no-skill counterfactual, intervenes on skill meaning, wording, identity, content, and assignment, and elicits attribution only after the answer is committed. We instantiate the framework as BACKROOMBench, a verified testbed spanning controlled logic and competition mathematics, multiple skill conditions, single-agent and multi-agent settings, and diverse model families. Our evaluation reveals a pervasive provenance failure. Across models and domains, stated skill use often remains stable while causal reliance and signed utility vary, producing both silent uptake and performative use. Behavioral effects follow procedural content more reliably than displayed skill identity, whereas stated attributions respond strongly to artifact availability. Observational detectors based on direct skill-use claims, text mentions, trace similarity, and an LLM judge do not identify which decisions actually depend on the skill. In multi-agent systems, skill influence can survive communication even after its source is lost, while no-skill teams still name skills and sources that were never supplied. These findings establish the Reasoning Backroom as a general AI provenance problem whose audit requires intervention.

</details>


### [224] [INCLAIR: Inception-Based Longitudinal Clinical Anomaly Detection with Informed Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27487)
*Maxx Richard Rahman,Wolfgang Maass*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出INCLAIR框架，用于在纵向临床档案中检测异常。该框架基于多个历史上下文对每个观测评分，在档案层面聚合证据，并在有限专家监督下生成可解释的自然语言解释。通过U统计量理论框架，INCLAIR实现了与档案长度无关的组合推断成本控制，并在三个临床数据集上一致优于现有基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 纵向临床档案中的异常检测在临床上重要但困难，主要挑战包括：异常证据通常稀疏、患者病历长度不一、专家标注（解释）成本高昂。现有方法难以有效应对这些挑战。

Method: INCLAIR框架包含三个核心组件：(1) 对每个观测基于多个历史上下文进行评分；(2) 在档案层面聚合异常证据；(3) 在有限专家监督下生成基于证据的自然语言解释。理论上，在档案内可交换性假设下，完整均值子序列分数呈现阶l的U统计量形式，从而获得方差分解和不完整子集近似，实现与档案长度无关的组合推断成本控制。同时分析发现均值聚合会衰减局部异常（衰减因子由异常支撑长度和档案长度决定），因此采用验证集选择的top-k池化策略来缓解该问题。

Result: 在三个临床数据集上，INCLAIR一致优于最先进的基线方法。在纵向类固醇档案的案例研究中，通过与领域专家评估（结合DNA分析支持）的对比，验证了INCLAIR的预测和解释的实用相关性。

Conclusion: INCLAIR在有限专家监督下实现了临床可操作的异常检测，既保证了统计上的理论严谨性（U统计量框架），又通过top-k池化克服了均值聚合对局部异常的衰减问题，同时提供可解释的自然语言输出，具有实际的临床应用价值。

Abstract: Detecting anomalies in longitudinal clinical profiles is clinically important but difficult: abnormal evidence is often sparse, patient histories have unequal length, and expert explanations are costly. We propose INCLAIR, a framework that scores each observation against multiple historical contexts, aggregates evidence at the profile level, and generates grounded natural-language explanations under limited expert supervision. Under stated within-profile exchangeability assumptions, the complete mean subsequence score takes an order-$l$ U-statistic form, yielding a variance decomposition and an incomplete-subset approximation that controls combinatorial inference cost independently of profile length. The same analysis shows that mean aggregation attenuates localized anomalies by a factor set by the anomaly support and profile length, motivating validation-selected top-$k$ pooling. Across three clinical datasets, INCLAIR consistently outperforms state-of-the-art baselines. We further validate practical relevance through a case study on longitudinal steroid profiles, comparing INCLAIR's predictions and explanations against domain-expert assessments supported by DNA analysis. The results show that INCLAIR enables clinically actionable anomaly detection under limited expert supervision.

</details>


### [225] [MedLLM: An Open Medical Language Model at the Sub-Billion Scale](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27490)
*Maxx Richard Rahman,Asim Ahmed,Mihan Mohagheghzadeh,Wolfgang Maass*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出MedLLM，一个0.1B参数的开源医学语言模型，通过三阶段训练流程（通用预训练、领域微调、偏好对齐）构建。该模型在次10亿参数规模下揭示了医学能力并非均匀退化，而是按任务类型分离：在上下文相关的QA任务中接近7B医学模型水平，但在知识回忆型QA上受限于模型容量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有开源医学语言模型都集中在7B参数或以上规模，次十亿参数（sub-billion）规模未得到充分研究。作者希望探索在小规模模型上医学能力如何表现，以及压缩对医学能力的影响模式。

Method: 采用完全开源的三阶段训练流程：(1) 通用预训练，使用课程式的序列长度调度；(2) 在MedFineWeb（一种基于嵌入相似性从通用网页数据中选取的参考引导医学语料）上进行领域微调；(3) 将SFT与直接偏好优化（DPO）结合的偏好对齐微调。

Result: 在医学基准测试中，MedLLM在上下文相关的问答任务上比医学适配的7B模型低2.9个百分点，并超过指令微调和通用7B基线；在知识回忆型问答上，在临床病例的MedQA上接近任务下限，但在MedMCQA上显著超过所有7B及以下基线。这种能力分离在7B规模被掩盖，只在容量稀缺时显现。

Conclusion: 次十亿参数的医学模型存在任务类型依赖的能力分离现象：上下文相关能力在小模型上保留较好，而知识回忆能力受限于模型容量而非适配程度。这一发现表明模型压缩对医学能力的影响具有任务特异性。

Abstract: Open medical language models have converged on a single scale: every widely used system runs at 7B parameters or more, leaving the sub-billion regime uncharacterized. We present MedLLM, an open 0.1B-parameter medical language model trained through a fully open three-phase pipeline: general pretraining with curriculum sequence-length scheduling, domain fine-tuning on MedFineWeb, a reference-guided medical corpus we release that is selected from general web data by embedding similarity to medical question-answering (QA) data, and preference-aligned fine-tuning combining SFT with direct preference optimization (DPO). Across medical benchmarks, MedLLM shows a pattern visible only at sub-billion scale: medical competence does not degrade uniformly under compression but splits by task type. On context-grounded QA it comes within $2.9$pp of a medically adapted 7B model and surpasses the instruction-tuned and general-purpose 7B baselines; on knowledge-recall QA it stays near the task floor on clinical-vignette MedQA yet significantly exceeds every 7B and sub-7B baseline on MedMCQA, indicating that where recall fails the constraint is model capacity rather than adaptation. This dissociation is masked at 7B, where both capabilities are present, and surfaces only when capacity is scarce.

</details>


### [226] [A dataset of rated conceptual arguments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27499)
*Emery Cooper,Caspar Oesterheld,Linh Chi Nguyen,Alexander Kastner,Ethan Perez*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文针对大语言模型在"概念性问题"（无标准答案、可通过论证辩论推进的问题）上的推理能力，引入了一个包含951条论证性批判、442篇立场文本的数据集，由六位专家在多个维度进行1458条评分，并提出两种评分函数对多种模型进行基准测试。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究主要关注LLM在可验证任务（如数学、编程）上的能力，但对概念性问题的推理能力知之甚少。概念性问题在哲学、AI安全、决策理论和社会选择等领域广泛存在，且底线结论难以评估，但个别情境化的论证可以被可靠评估。

Method: 引入包含951条论证性批判和442篇立场文本的数据集，涵盖AI安全、决策理论、伦理和政治等主题；由六位专家在中心性、强度、正确性和清晰度等维度上进行1458条评分；提出两种评分函数并对多种模型进行基准测试。

Result: 模型在该数据集上的表现与其总体能力排名一致，即能力更强的模型在概念性问题论证评估上也表现更好。

Conclusion: 通过评估个别论证而非整体结论，可以对概念性问题上的模型能力进行更可靠的测量；当前模型性能与总体能力排名一致，说明概念性问题推理可能依赖通用的推理能力。

Abstract: Large language models have improved rapidly on tasks with verifiable answers, such as mathematics and programming. Much less is known about their ability to reason about what we call conceptual questions: questions for which no ground truth is realistically accessible and no widely accepted resolution methodology exists, but on which progress can still be made by debating arguments. Most philosophical questions are of this kind, as are central components of questions in AI safety, decision theory, and social choice. Our approach is based on the view that while bottom-line conclusions on such questions are hard to evaluate, individual contextualized arguments can be evaluated far more reliably. We therefore introduce a dataset of 951 argumentative critiques of 442 position texts, spanning topics from AI safety and decision theory to ethics and politics, with 1,458 ratings by six expert raters along dimensions including centrality, strength, correctness, and clarity. We propose two scoring functions and benchmark a range of models. Performance tracks general capability rankings.

</details>


### [227] [Automated Transcript Analysis for Detecting Flaws in Agentic Benchmarks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27518)
*Jeff Mohl,Nelson Gardner-Challis,Magda Dubois,Harry Coppock,Benjamin Allan-Rahill,Kaelan Yim,Damian Sójka,James Mann,Justin Olive*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文开发了AI扫描器用于自动检测agentic基准测试中的四类有效性问题（ground truth访问、工具故障、猜测漏洞、答案格式歧义），并在Inspect Evals基准测试集上与人工标注进行比较，成功在五个广泛使用的基准中发现了质量问题，验证了自动化审计基准测试质量的可行性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 前沿模型的能力常通过agentic基准测试评估，但基准测试必须准确无误才能保证评估结果可信。以往人工审计（如SWE-Bench-Verified）发现过有效性问题，但人工审查难以扩展，且不确定自动化方法能否可靠地发现影响基准有效性的缺陷。

Method: 开发了AI扫描器，针对四类有效性问题（ground truth访问、工具故障、猜测漏洞、答案格式歧义）设计检测方法；为每类问题制定评分标准供人工标注；在Inspect Evals基准测试的留出测试集上将扫描器结果与人工标注进行比较验证。

Result: 扫描器在五个广泛使用的基准中发现了多个已验证的质量问题，包括随机人工检查难以发现的案例。但并非所有问题都被识别出来，且扫描器性能在不同基准、标准和模型之间存在显著差异。

Conclusion: 研究证明了使用自动化转录分析来审计基准质量的可行性，但指出了若干开放挑战（如评估领域标准化差距）需要解决，以提升扫描器的质量保证能力。

Abstract: Capabilities of frontier models are often assessed using agentic benchmarks. To trust these results, benchmarks must accurately measure what they claim to and be free from invalidating flaws. Previous manual audits of benchmarks such as SWE-Bench-Verified have uncovered several validity issues in transcripts. However, manual review is difficult to scale, and it is unclear whether automated methods can reliably surface flaws that compromise benchmark validity. In this paper, we developed AI scanners to detect four types of validity issues: ground truth access, tool failure, guessing vulnerability, and answer format ambiguity. We produced grading rubrics for each to instruct human labeling, and evaluated the scanners against human labels on a held-out test set of Inspect Evals benchmarks. Our scanners identified several verified quality issues in five widely used benchmarks, including cases unlikely to be caught by random manual inspection. Not all cases were identified, and scanner performance varied substantially across benchmarks, criteria and models. We highlight several open challenges to be addressed to improve scanners for stronger quality assurance claims, including broader standardization gaps in the evaluation field that degrade scanner performance. Together, these results serve as a proof of concept for using automated transcript analysis to audit benchmark quality more broadly.

</details>


### [228] [AI Literacy: An Exercise in Power-Knowledge](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27547)
*Brady D. Lund,Zoë Abbie Teel*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文批判现有AI素养框架过于强调技术能力和"负责任使用"原则，导致学习者沦为AI的"消费者"而非真正的认知主体。基于福柯的权力-知识理论、弗莱雷的批判意识教育学及数字素养研究，作者提出将AI素养重新概念化为一种批判性实践，并构建了包含"情境化使用、批判性审视、参与式治理"三部分的理论框架，旨在培养具备认知能动性的"行动者"而非信息消费者。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着生成式AI成为社会知识生产的重要系统，谁有权访问和塑造这种生产变得日益重要。现有AI素养框架由技术能力和负责任使用原则主导，强化了"消费者"取向，限制了学习者的真正认知能动性（epistemic agency）。此外，AI工具的社会获取不平等复现了长期的认知不公正（epistemic injustice），而现有素养框架未能充分回应这一结构性不平等。

Method: 本文为理论性/概念性论文，采用批判理论视角，综合运用三种理论资源：（1）福柯的权力-知识概念，分析AI系统中知识生产与权力结构的交织；（2）弗莱雷的批判意识教育学，强调通过批判性反思实现解放；（3）数字素养学术研究，作为连接理论与实践的基础。在此基础上提出一个三部分AI素养框架：情境化使用（contextual use）、批判性审视（critical interrogation）、参与式治理（participatory governance）。

Result: 论文提出了一个以认知赋权为导向的三部分AI素养框架，将AI素养定位为培养"认知行动者"（epistemic agents）而非"称职的信息消费者"。该框架涵盖情境化使用、批判性审视和参与式治理三个维度，并要求框架必须回应结构性不平等，以实现真正的赋权。

Conclusion: 文章结论认为，AI素养不应停留在技术操作和风险规避层面，而应成为一种批判性实践，使个体不仅能使用AI，还能批判性地评估AI、抵抗其结构性假设，并参与AI治理。只有将结构不平等纳入考量、以赋权为导向的素养框架，才能实现从"消费者"到"认知行动者"的转变。

Abstract: As generative artificial intelligence becomes one of the most significant systems of knowledge production in our society today, questions relating to who can access and shape that production grow increasingly important in our discourse. This paper argues that the existing frameworks for AI literacy, which are dominated by technical competency and responsible-use principles, are insufficient because they enforce a "consumer" orientation toward AI rather than fostering genuine epistemic agency. Based upon Foucault's concept of power-knowledge, Freire's pedagogy of critical consciousness, and scholarship of digital literacy, this paper proposes a reconceptualization of AI literacy as a critical practice that equips individuals not just to use AI systems, but to critically evaluate them, resist their structuring assumptions, and participate in their governance. The paper further argues that unequal access to AI tools in society recapitulates longstanding epistemic injustices, and that a literacy framework oriented toward empowerment must account for these structural inequities. A three-part framework of AI literacy based on the notions of contextual use, critical interrogation, and participatory governance frames this literacy as a cultivation of epistemic "agents" rather than the training of competent consumers of AI-generated information.

</details>


### [229] [Using Large Language Models for Idea Generation in Innovation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27553)
*Lennart Meincke,Karan Girotra,Gideon Nave,Christian Terwiesch,Karl T. Ulrich*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文评估了大型语言模型（LLM）在新产品创意生成中的有效性，将GPT-4生成的创意与大学生人工创意进行对比，发现AI生成创意在平均购买意向和顶尖创意质量上显著优于人工创意，但新颖性和多样性较差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索大型语言模型（LLM）是否能够有效地生成新产品创意，特别是在新产品开发中，AI的创造力是否可以替代或超越人类创造力，这是一个尚未被充分验证的重要问题。

Method: 将创意分为三组：大学生人工创意、GPT-4零样本提示生成的创意、GPT-4少样本提示生成的创意。使用市场调研技术预测购买意向概率，用文本挖掘评估创意相似性，用人评分员评估新颖性，并比较顶尖10%创意的质量。

Result: AI生成创意在平均购买意向上优于人工创意，少样本略优于零样本；但AI创意新颖性较低且相似性更高，多样性不足；在顶尖10%创意的排名中，AI创意入选概率是人工创意的7倍。

Conclusion: 尽管AI在创意多样性和新颖性方面存在缺陷，但AI创造力在生成高质量新产品创意方面具有显著优势，7:1的优势是保守估计，实际优势更大。

Abstract: This research evaluates the efficacy of large language models (LLMs) in generating new product ideas. To do so, we compare three pools of ideas for new products targeted toward college students and priced at 50 dollars or less. The first pool of ideas was created by university students in a product design course before the availability of LLMs. The second and third pools of ideas were generated by GPT-4 from OpenAI using zero-shot and few-shot prompting, respectively. We evaluated idea quality using standard market research techniques to predict average purchase intent probability. We used text mining to assess idea similarity and human raters to evaluate idea novelty. We find that AI-generated ideas outperform human-generated ideas in terms of average purchase intent, with few-shot prompting yielding slightly higher intent than zero-shot prompting. However, AI-generated ideas are perceived as less novel and exhibit higher pairwise similarity, particularly with few-shot prompting, indicating a less diverse solution landscape. When focusing on the quality of the best ideas rather than the average ideas, we find that AI-generated ideas are seven times more likely to rank among the top 10 percent of ideas, demonstrating a significant advantage over human-generated ideas. We propose that this seven-to-one advantage is a conservative estimate because it does not account for the greater productivity of AI. Our findings suggest that despite some drawbacks, AI creativity presents a substantial benefit in generating high-quality ideas for new product development.

</details>


### [230] [Evaluating Agentic Bioinformatics through Function, Evidence, and Validation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27556)
*Phuc Pham,Truong-Son Hy*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出函数-证据-验证（FEV）框架，以可审计的工作流轨迹而非最终输出作为主要分析单元，系统评估生物信息学大语言模型代理的科学可信度，并基于109个代理系统与28个基准资源（涵盖128篇出版物）的映射，指出当前代理在规划与工具执行方面进展显著，但在可重放性、来源追溯、科学评估与外验证方面仍严重不足。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有大语言模型代理在生物学分析中日益普及，但流畅的响应、成功的工具调用和基准分数并不能证明科学可信度。已有综述主要按应用、架构和代理能力组织，缺乏对代理生成工作流问责性的统一操作化评估框架。

Method: 引入FEV框架，将可检查的工作流轨迹作为首要分析单元，从三个维度评估：函数（展示的工作流操作）、证据（对操作和主张的可追溯支持）、验证（特定用例的验证）。基于该框架对109个代理或代理邻近系统及28个基准/评估资源进行系统映射分析。

Result: 横跨基因组学、单细胞与空间组学、蛋白质科学、药物发现、计算病理学和通用生物信息学自动化等领域，发现规划的成熟度普遍高于可重放性、来源追溯、稳健科学评估、外部验证和前瞻性实证测试。代理系统在科学问责相关维度上发展不均衡。

Conclusion: 生物信息学代理应通过工作流正确性而非仅最终答案正确性来评估。FEV为比较不同系统、设计透明、可审计且科学问责的生物信息学工作流提供了实用基础。

Abstract: Large language model agents increasingly plan, execute, and interpret biological analyses, yet fluent responses, successful tool calls, and benchmark performance alone do not establish scientific credibility. Existing reviews primarily organize biological agents by application, architecture, and agentic capability, but do not jointly operationalize the accountability of agent-generated workflows. We address this gap by treating the inspectable workflow trajectory, rather than architecture or final output alone, as the primary unit of analysis. We introduce the Function--Evidence--Validation (FEV) framework, which separates demonstrated workflow operations, traceable support for actions and claims, and use-case-specific validation. Using FEV, we map 109 agentic or agent-adjacent systems and 28 benchmark or evaluation resources, representing 128 unique publications across genomics, single-cell and spatial omics, protein science, drug discovery, computational pathology, and general bioinformatics automation. Across domains, planning and tool-mediated execution have advanced more rapidly than replayability, provenance, robust scientific assessment, external validation, and prospective empirical testing. We therefore argue that agentic bioinformatics should be assessed through workflow correctness rather than final-answer correctness alone. FEV provides a practical basis for comparing systems and designing transparent, auditable, and scientifically accountable bioinformatics workflows.

</details>


### [231] [DeepResearch Agent System](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27562)
*Yong Huang,Yulu Huang,for the team Collaboration*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文介绍了一个名为DeepResearch Agent System的大型语言模型系统，采用稀疏激活架构（300亿总参数/30亿激活参数），专为深度信息检索、多步推理和自主研究任务设计。系统在多个智能体搜索基准上取得SOTA性能，推理速度比同等规模稠密模型快3.2倍，并支持128K上下文窗口、双模式推理引擎、多工具协调和GRPO强化学习优化。系统已完全开源。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的语言模型在处理深度信息检索和自主研究任务时面临挑战：稠密模型的推理效率低、长上下文处理能力有限、多步推理和多工具协调能力不足。论文旨在构建一个高效、高性能的智能体系统来解决这些问题。

Method: 1) 采用稀疏激活架构（300亿参数，每token仅激活30亿参数）提升推理效率；2) 128K上下文窗口配以层级注意力机制；3) 双模式推理引擎：ReAct范式用于基础多步问题求解，IterResearch模式用于高性能迭代研究（最多20步推理）；4) 多工具协调集成检索、计算、网络搜索和文件解析模块；5) 基于GRPO算法的强化学习优化框架提供token级策略梯度；6) 自动化数据合成流水线采用基于种子扩展的方法。

Result: 推理速度比稠密模型快3.2倍；长上下文准确率提升18.7%、召回率提升23.4%；多步推理准确率提升31.2%；工具使用准确率达92.1%；训练稳定性提升35%、收敛速度加快42%；数据合成可用率达92.5%；在Humanity's Last Exam、BrowserComp Chinese、WebWalkerQA基准上分别达到87.3%、85.3%和91.2%。

Conclusion: DeepResearch Agent System通过稀疏激活、多模式推理和强化学习优化等技术创新，在推理效率、长上下文处理、多工具协调和研究任务性能方面均取得显著突破，并已完全开源，适用于学术研究、商业分析、研发支持和教育等多种应用场景。

Abstract: The DeepResearch Agent System is a large language model system engineered for deep information retrieval, multi-step reasoning, and autonomous research tasks. Built upon a sparse activation architecture with 30 billion total parameters of which only 3 billion are activated per token, the system achieves state-of-the-art performance on multiple agent search benchmarks while delivering 3.2 times faster inference compared to dense counterparts of equivalent scale. The system supports a 128K-token context window with hierarchical attention mechanisms that yield 18.7% accuracy and 23.4% recall improvements over standard long-context approaches. A dual-mode reasoning engine provides both a ReAct paradigm for basic multi-step problem solving and an IterResearch mode for high-performance iterative research with up to 20 reasoning steps, collectively delivering a 31.2% accuracy improvement over single-pass baselines. Multi-tool coordination integrates retrieval, computation, web search, and file parsing modules to achieve 92.1% tool-use accuracy. A reinforcement learning optimization framework based on the GRPO algorithm provides token-level policy gradients that improve training stability by 35% and accelerate convergence by 42%. An automated data synthesis pipeline with seed-based expansion achieves a 92.5% usability rate. Benchmark results include 87.3% on Humanity's Last Exam, 85.3% on BrowserComp Chinese, and 91.2% on WebWalkerQA. The system is fully open-sourced, including data synthesis, training, and inference code, and supports applications in academic research, business analysis, R&D support, and education.

</details>


### [232] [What makes prompts a graph: necessary and sufficient conditions for prompt graph engineering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27578)
*Sandeco Macedo*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文针对提示词工程领域缺乏统一术语的问题，提出"提示图工程"(Prompt Graph Engineering)的构成性定义。该定义包含四个条件：显式结构、结构与提示内容分离、可执行语义、图作为一等工程工件。作者通过概念分析和灰色文献梳理了该概念的谱系，并用包含/排除测试应用于六个真实系统（LangGraph、DSPy、Prompt Flow、AutoGen、CrewAI、Claude Code subagents）验证其一致性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 工业界实践已经广泛使用图结构来组织提示词（如LangGraph、DSPy等框架），但学术词汇却滞后于实践。"图"这一概念在不同语境下含义混乱——可能指采样策略内的推理拓扑、多智能体对话或编排工件，而"提示工程"仍然让人联想到写一句好的字符串。缺乏统一的参考定义来将提示词视为显式、可执行、可改进的图节点。

Method: 方法包括：(1) 对带持久标识符的来源进行概念分析，辅以主要灰色文献；(2) 重构概念谱系：从数据流图和构建系统，到提示链和思维拓扑（链、树、图），再到被编译和优化的图工件；(3) 提出提示图工程的定义及四个构成条件；(4) 将条件操作化为包含/排除测试；(5) 对照六个邻近概念划定边界，并对六个真实系统应用测试进行验证。

Result: 提出了包含四个条件的构成性定义（显式结构、结构-内容分离、可执行语义、图作为一等工件），并操作化为包含/排除测试。该测试对六个真实系统（LangGraph、DSPy、Prompt Flow、AutoGen、CrewAI、Claude Code subagents）的应用结果一致，同时能清晰地将六个邻近概念排除在定义之外。最后提出了沿四个设计张力轴的研究议程。

Conclusion: 本文的贡献是为工业界已在日常实践中使用但未被精确命名的实践提供了一个操作性定义和共享词汇表。这不仅填补了术语空白，还为提示图工程的进一步研究（沿四个设计张力轴）奠定了概念基础。

Abstract: Prompts stopped being isolated strings some time ago. In real systems, one model call feeds another, retrieval interleaves with generation, routers branch, and aggregators merge parallel results. Practice converged on a single structure to hold this together: the graph. Frameworks such as LangGraph, DSPy, and Prompt Flow expose it openly, and research systems already optimize it automatically. The vocabulary, however, lags behind. Graph names, variously, a reasoning topology inside one sampling strategy, a multi-agent conversation, or an orchestration artifact, while prompt engineering still evokes writing one good string. What is missing is a reference definition treating prompts as nodes of an explicit, executable, improvable graph. We build that definition through conceptual analysis over sources with persistent identifiers, complemented by primary grey literature. We reconstruct the genealogy of the idea, from dataflow graphs and build systems, through prompt chaining and the thought topologies (chain, tree, graph), to graphs compiled and optimized as artifacts. We then propose a constitutive definition of prompt graph engineering, state its four conditions (explicit structure, separation between structure and prompt content, executable semantics, and the graph as a first-class engineering artifact), and operationalize them as an inclusion and exclusion test. We draw the boundary against six neighboring concepts and apply the test to six real systems (LangGraph, DSPy, Prompt Flow, AutoGen, CrewAI, and Claude Code subagents); it includes and excludes consistently. We close with a research agenda organized along four design tension axes. The contribution is an operational definition and a shared vocabulary for a practice that industry already exercises daily without naming precisely.

</details>


### [233] [From Minds to Models: The Intersection of Psychology and LLM Behaviours](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27579)
*Oliver Guidetti,Reza Ryan*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本研究基于隐式联想测验的提示改编，测试了ChatGPT（GPT-3.5T、GPT-4、GPT-4T）在开放式文本中是否在不同种族条件下产生情感差异。通过126个提示获得378个响应，双因素方差分析发现种族条件的主效应很小且边缘显著（p=.042），但在秩变换敏感性分析和Tukey校正后均不显著，表明证据薄弱且依赖于分析方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM的决策过程复杂、非线性且难以解释，常被与人类心智相提并论。为了在政府和医疗等高风险领域安全部署LLM，需要借助心理学方法（如隐式联想测验）来评估模型行为中可能存在的隐式种族偏见。

Method: 将14个基础问题与8个种族类别及1个无种族对照交叉，生成126个提示；每个提示分别提交给GPT-3.5T、GPT-4和GPT-4T，共获得378个响应。情感评分采用分类标签与源分数结合的方式：正向标签保留源分数，负向标签取负值，中性响应记为零。使用双因素方差分析检验种族条件和模型的影响。

Result: 双因素方差分析显示种族条件有小的主效应（F(8,351)=2.04, p=.042, partial-eta squared=.044），但无模型主效应和交互作用。然而，秩变换敏感性分析不显著（p=.145），Tukey校正后无显著配对差异。仅未校正的欧洲与原住民澳大利亚人比较显著，但这是事后选择的结果，仅作为假设生成性发现。

Conclusion: ChatGPT在不同种族条件下产生情感差异的证据微弱且依赖于分析方法。当前情感评分方法无法区分评价性偏见与提示所引发的历史内容的效价。研究建议进行设计改进，并呼吁跨学科发展模型偏见的行测度量方法。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are often compared with the human mind because their decision-making is complex, non-linear and difficult to interpret. Psychological methods developed to investigate unobservable mental processes may therefore help examine LLM behaviour, particularly in government and healthcare. Building on prompt-based adaptations of the Implicit Association Test, this study tested whether ChatGPT produced sentiment differences across racial conditions in open-ended text.
